~~~python
B, EXT = 'B', 'out-of-boundary'
HALF = F(1, 2)

def gate_reads(G, s, j):
    return frozenset([('v', j)] + [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]])

def Ev(G, o, sigma, t, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Event witness of Definition 5 (a, κ, η are certified separately; see gate_criteria.py). Uses only the pre-beat state o and the letter σ. Returns {id: dict}."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    out = {}
    def add(kind, idx, car, tgt, c, reads):
        eid = (kind, idx, t)
        assert eid not in out
        out[eid] = dict(id=eid, t=t, car=car, tgt=tgt, c=c, reads=frozenset(reads))
    vt = [G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= G.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(G.n_out)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
        add('LEAK', (j,), B, ('v', j), (G.lam[j] - 1) * v[j], {('v', j)})
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            if s[i]:
                add('SYN', (i, j), B, ('v', j), w[i][j], {('w', i, j)})
        if I[j] != 0:
            add('INJ', (j,), EXT, ('v', j), F(I[j]), set())
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), B, ('v', j), G.vreset[j] - vt[j], gr)
            add('YIN', (j,), B, ('y', j), F(1), gr)
        add('YDEC', (j,), B, ('y', j), (G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j], {('y', j)})
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        add('XDEC', (i,), B, ('x', i), (G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i], {('x', i)})
        if s[i]:
            add('XIN', (i,), B, ('x', i), F(1), set())
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            gr = gate_reads(G, s, j)
            S = w[i][j]
            if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                c = G.Ap * (G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i]); S += c
                add('PLUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('x', i)})
            if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                c = -G.Am * (G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j]); S += c
                add('MINUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, gr | {('y', j)})
            if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                c = -G.rho * (w[i][j] - G.b[i][j]); S += c
                add('RELAX', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), c, {('w', i, j)})
            cl = min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], S))
            if cl != S:
                add('CLIP', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), cl - S, gr | {('w', i, j), ('x', i), ('y', j)})
    return out

def slot_deps(G, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Definition 7: slot dependency. For a given letter (independent of the state), returns {post-state node n: set of pre-state nodes}, i.e. every pre-beat node on which n(t+1) depends:
    the union of the read sets of all event slots pointing to n (whether each occurs is fixed by the state), plus the node itself for Structural Positions (persistence edge)."""
    s = G.pat[sigma[0]]
    dep = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        gr = set(gate_reads(G, s, j))
        dep[('v', j)] = set(gr)                 # LEAK reads v; SYN reads active w; RESET slot reads gr
        dep[('y', j)] = {('y', j)} | gr         # YDEC reads y; YIN slot reads gr
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        dep[('x', i)] = {('x', i)}
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            d = {('w', i, j)}                   # persistence
            if plastic and bap:
                d |= set(gate_reads(G, s, j)) | {('x', i)}   # PLUS slot
                if s[i]:
                    d |= {('y', j)}                          # MINUS slot
            d |= {('x', i), ('y', j)} if plastic and bap else set()   # CLIP slot (upper bound by union)
            dep[('w', i, j)] = d
    return dep

def slot_reads(G, e):
    """Slot read set of event e (the pre-beat nodes on which its occurrence and component depend; identical to the reads given by Ev, listed separately to show that it depends only on the letter)."""
    return e['reads']

def node_val(o, k):
    w, v, x, y = o
    return w[k[1]][k[2]] if k[0] == 'w' else {'v': v, 'x': x, 'y': y}[k[0]][k[1]]

def nodes(G):
    return [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)] + [('v', j) for j in range(G.n_out)] + \
           [('x', i) for i in range(G.n_in)] + [('y', j) for j in range(G.n_out)]

def set_nodes(o, assign):
    """Writes assign = {node: value} into the state (returns a new state)."""
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]; v, x, y = list(o[1]), list(o[2]), list(o[3])
    for k, val in assign.items():
        if k[0] == 'w': w[k[1]][k[2]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'v': v[k[1]] = val
        elif k[0] == 'x': x[k[1]] = val
        else: y[k[1]] = val
    return (w, v, x, y)

def apply_events(G, o, evs):
    """Declared form of K_t: post-state = pre-state + the sum of all event components on the same node (Lemma R1)."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    w2 = [r[:] for r in w]; v2, x2, y2 = list(v), list(x), list(y)
    for e in evs.values():
        k = e['tgt']
        if k[0] == 'w': w2[k[1]][k[2]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'v': v2[k[1]] += e['c']
        elif k[0] == 'x': x2[k[1]] += e['c']
        else: y2[k[1]] += e['c']
    return (w2, v2, x2, y2)

def ev_from_log(G, o, ev, t, plastic, bap, rho):
    """Generates the witness independently from the snn_core log (s_in, s_out, comp, teach) and the pre-beat state: occurrence is taken from the firings in the log and the protocol switches,
    plasticity components from the comp of the log, and clipping events from the difference between the comp net value and the sum of the three components."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    s, g, I = ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'], ev['teach']
    out = {}
    def add(kind, idx, car, tgt, c):
        out[(kind, idx, t)] = dict(id=(kind, idx, t), t=t, car=car, tgt=tgt, c=c)
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        vt = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
        add('LEAK', (j,), B, ('v', j), (G.lam[j] - 1) * v[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            if s[i]: add('SYN', (i, j), B, ('v', j), w[i][j])
        if I[j] != 0: add('INJ', (j,), EXT, ('v', j), F(I[j]))
        if g[j]:
            add('RESET', (j,), B, ('v', j), G.vreset[j] - vt); add('YIN', (j,), B, ('y', j), F(1))
        add('YDEC', (j,), B, ('y', j), (G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        add('XDEC', (i,), B, ('x', i), (G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
        if s[i]: add('XIN', (i,), B, ('x', i), F(1))
    for (i, j), (pp, pm, pr, net) in ev['comp'].items():
        if plastic and bap and g[j]: add('PLUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pp)
        if plastic and bap and s[i]: add('MINUS', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pm)
        if plastic and rho != 0: add('RELAX', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), pr)
        if net != pp + pm + pr: add('CLIP', (i, j), B, ('w', i, j), net - (pp + pm + pr))
    return out

def d13_edges(G, evs, t):
    """D13: edges of the actual evidence-path graph of this beat (read edges (n, t) → e, write edges e → (tgt, t+1), Structural-Position persistence edges (w, t) → (w, t+1)), with times."""
    E = []
    for e in evs.values():
        for k in e['reads']:
            E.append(((k, F(t)), (e['id'], F(t) + HALF)))
        E.append(((e['id'], F(t) + HALF), (e['tgt'], F(t + 1))))
    for k in nodes(G):
        if k[0] == 'w':
            E.append(((k, F(t)), (k, F(t + 1))))
    return E

def check_history(S, Gx, hist, o0=None, plastic=True, bap=True, rho=F(0), T=None):
    st = S.init() if o0 is None else dict(w=[r[:] for r in o0[0]], v=list(o0[1]), x=list(o0[2]), y=list(o0[3]))
    o = (st['w'], st['v'], st['x'], st['y'])
    stats = dict(ticks=0, events=0, clip=0, closure_bad=0, gen_bad=0, d4_bad=0, d13_bad=0, edges=0, d7_bad=0)
    all_ids = set()
    steps = []
    N = set(nodes(Gx))
    for t, (inp, teach) in enumerate(hist):
        evs = Ev(Gx, o, (inp, teach), t, plastic, bap)
        st, ev = S.step(st, inp, teach, plastic=plastic, bap=bap, relax=rho)
        o_next = (st['w'], st['v'], st['x'], st['y'])
        evl = ev_from_log(Gx, o, ev, t, plastic, bap, rho)
        if set(evs) != set(evl) or any(evs[k]['c'] != evl[k]['c'] or evs[k]['car'] != evl[k]['car'] or
                                       evs[k]['tgt'] != evl[k]['tgt'] for k in evs):
            stats['gen_bad'] += 1
        if apply_events(Gx, o, evs) != o_next:
            stats['closure_bad'] += 1
        for e in evs.values():   # D4: distinct identifiers, carrier range, single action target in the node set, time
            if e['id'] in all_ids or e['car'] not in (B, EXT) or e['tgt'] not in N or e['t'] != t:
                stats['d4_bad'] += 1
            all_ids.add(e['id'])
        for (u, tu), (vv, tv) in d13_edges(Gx, evs, t):   # D13: edges point forward in time, endpoint types valid
            stats['edges'] += 1
            okU = (u in N) or (u in evs)
            okV = (vv in N) or (vv in evs)
            if not (tu < tv) or not okU or not okV:
                stats['d13_bad'] += 1
        Omega = [dict(id=e['id'], t=t, car=e['car'], tgt=e['tgt'], c=e['c'], a=None, kappa=None, eta=None) for e in evs.values()]
        step = dict(t=t, o_before=o, o_after=o_next, Omega=Omega, Pi=[])   # D7: Π_t is empty (no pulse acts on Structural Positions)
        if any(len(om) != 8 for om in Omega) or step['Pi']:
            stats['d7_bad'] += 1
        steps.append(step)
        stats['events'] += len(evs); stats['clip'] += sum(1 for e in evs.values() if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP')
        stats['ticks'] += 1
        o = o_next
    rpath = dict(X=[k for k in nodes(Gx) if k[0] == 'w'], Trace=None, Ident=None, steps=steps, T=T)
    return stats, rpath

def readset_check(G_list, n=1500, seed=7):
    """Finite verification of Lemma R2: set every node outside the read set to a random valid value; the occurrence and component of the event stay unchanged (clipping events checked as well)."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    trials, viol, clip_trials = 0, 0, 0
    for _ in range(n):
        rnd = lambda hi_: F(rng.randint(0, 32), 16) * hi_ / 2
        o = ([[rnd(2) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [rnd(3) for _ in range(2)], [rnd(2) for _ in range(4)], [rnd(2) for _ in range(2)])
        sig = (rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv)))
        Gx = rng.choice(G_list)
        evs = Ev(Gx, o, sig, 0)
        for eid, e in evs.items():
            keep = e['reads']
            o2 = set_nodes(o, {k: (rnd(3) if k[0] == 'v' else rnd(2)) for k in nodes(Gx) if k not in keep})
            evs2 = Ev(Gx, o2, sig, 0)
            trials += 1
            clip_trials += eid[0] == 'CLIP'
            if eid not in evs2 or evs2[eid]['c'] != e['c']:
                viol += 1
    return trials, clip_trials, viol

def reach_forward(G, hist, sources, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Forward reachability of Definition 7: sources = {(node, beat)}; returns R[t] = set of reachable nodes in the pre-beat state of beat t (including the sources of that beat)."""
    R = {}
    cur = set()
    for t in range(len(hist) + 1):
        cur = set(cur) | {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        R[t] = frozenset(cur)
        if t == len(hist):
            break
        dep = slot_deps(G, hist[t], plastic, bap)
        cur = {n for n, d in dep.items() if d & R[t]}
    return R

def noninterference_check(G_list, n=400, seed=11):
    """Finite verification of Lemma R3: take any source node set S (any nodes, any beats) and apply any assignment on S;
    the values of nodes outside the forward-reachable set, and the events whose read sets lie outside the reachable set (occurrence and component), are all unchanged."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    stats = dict(runs=0, node_checks=0, ev_checks=0, viol=0)
    for _ in range(n):
        Gx = rng.choice(G_list)
        L = rng.randint(4, 16)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(L)]
        o0 = ([[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        srcs = {(rng.choice(nodes(Gx)), rng.randint(0, L - 1)) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))}
        vals = {sk: (F(rng.randint(0, 24), 8)) for sk in srcs}
        R = reach_forward(Gx, hist, srcs)
        o, oc = o0, o0
        for t in range(L):
            oc = set_nodes(oc, {k: vals[(k, u)] for (k, u) in srcs if u == t})
            for k in nodes(Gx):
                if k not in R[t]:
                    stats['node_checks'] += 1
                    if node_val(o, k) != node_val(oc, k):
                        stats['viol'] += 1
            ea, ec = Ev(Gx, o, hist[t], t), Ev(Gx, oc, hist[t], t)
            for eid in set(ea) | set(ec):
                rd = (ea.get(eid) or ec.get(eid))['reads']
                if not (rd & R[t]):
                    stats['ev_checks'] += 1
                    if (eid in ea) != (eid in ec) or (eid in ea and ea[eid]['c'] != ec[eid]['c']):
                        stats['viol'] += 1
            o, _ = Gx.step(o, hist[t]); oc, _ = Gx.step(oc, hist[t])
        stats['runs'] += 1
    return stats

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    EPS = F(1, 100)
    T = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('none', (0, 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6
    REST = [('none', (0, 0))] * 8
    WIN = GC.WIN0()
    HIST = FORM + REST + WIN
    t0 = len(FORM) + len(REST)
    print('1. Declaration (main text, Definitions 4–7)')
    print('  O_t = (w, v, x, y); Structural Positions X = 8 synapses; T = {w00, w10} (entries 0, 1 → output 0); ε =', EPS)
    print('  event kinds: LEAK_j, SYN_ij, INJ_j (out-of-boundary), RESET_j, YIN_j, YDEC_j, XDEC_i, XIN_i, PLUS_ij, MINUS_ij, RELAX_ij, CLIP_ij')
    print('  declared history: FORM(18) + REST(8) + WIN(18), W = beats', t0, 'to', t0 + len(WIN) - 1, '(counted from 0); Π_t = ∅ (no out-of-boundary pulse acts on Structural Positions)')
    print('2. R-layer witnesses generated from the snn_core run log and checked beat by beat (K_t agreement of Lemma R1, D4, D7, D13)')
    tot = []
    def show(label, stt):
        print(f'  {label}: {stt["ticks"]} beats, event witnesses {stt["events"]} (clipping {stt["clip"]}), D13 edges {stt["edges"]}; '
              f'mismatches between the two generations {stt["gen_bad"]}; K_t mismatches {stt["closure_bad"]}; D4 violations {stt["d4_bad"]}; '
              f'D7 violations {stt["d7_bad"]}; D13 violations {stt["d13_bad"]}')
        tot.append(stt)
    stt, rpath = check_history(S, G, HIST, T=T); show('s1 frozen declared history', stt)
    st_r = S.run(REST, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o_er = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    show('erasure-side WIN (R1(a′))', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_er)[0])
    o_r = (st_r['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    show('bap off', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_r, bap=False)[0])
    show('plastic off', check_history(S, G, WIN, o0=o_r, plastic=False)[0])
    G10 = GC.snn_gate(S, F(1, 10))
    show('RELAX = 1/10', check_history(S, G10, HIST, rho=F(1, 10))[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    sat = ([('AB', (F(2), F(2)))] * 6 + [('A', (0, 0))] * 6 + [('B', (F(2), 0)), ('none', (0, 0))] * 6) * 2
    show('saturation history (A_PLUS = A_MINUS = 1/2)', check_history(S, Gs, sat)[0])
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    rng = random.Random(1101)
    tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)]
    agg = dict(ticks=0, events=0, clip=0, closure_bad=0, gen_bad=0, d4_bad=0, d13_bad=0, edges=0, d7_bad=0)
    for _ in range(100):
        rho = rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)])
        Gr = GC.snn_gate(S, rho)
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(50)]
        w0 = [[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)]
        o0 = (w0, [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        stt_r, _ = check_history(S, Gr, hist, o0=o0, plastic=rng.random() < .9, bap=rng.random() < .9, rho=rho)
        for k in agg: agg[k] += stt_r[k]
    show('100 random histories (seed 1101)', agg)
    print(f'  RPath (s1): X = 8 positions, T = {T}, {len(rpath["steps"])} witness steps; Trace and Ident are evidence values, computed by gate_criteria.py')
    print('3. Read-set sufficiency (Lemma R2; all nodes outside the read set perturbed to random values; seed 7; clipping events included)')
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 2), F(1, 2)
    Gsat = GC.snn_gate(S)
    S.A_PLUS, S.A_MINUS = F(1, 8), F(1, 16)
    trials, ctr, viol = readset_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {trials} event–perturbation pairs ({ctr} of them clipping events), violations {viol}')
    print('4. Non-interference lemma (Lemma R3): outside the forward-reachable set of the slot-dependency graph, arbitrary assignment interventions change no node value and no event (seed 11)')
    ni = noninterference_check([G, G10, Gsat])
    print(f'  {ni["runs"]} random histories with random source sets; node comparisons {ni["node_checks"]}, event comparisons {ni["ev_checks"]}, violations {ni["viol"]}')
    bad = sum(s_['closure_bad'] + s_['gen_bad'] + s_['d4_bad'] + s_['d13_bad'] + s_['d7_bad'] for s_ in tot)
    print('5. Conclusion (finite verification):', 'on all histories K_t agrees beat by beat, the two witness generations are identical, and D4, D7, D13 show no violations; read-set sufficiency and non-interference show no violations either.'
          if bad == 0 and viol == 0 and ni['viol'] == 0 else f'violations found: {bad} / {viol} / {ni["viol"]}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.4  lp\_exact.py  Exact rational linear feasibility (verbatim identical to Appendix A.5 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0)

~~~python
"""Exact rational linear feasibility (Fourier–Motzkin, strict inequalities included): a witness point when feasible, a Motzkin certificate when infeasible; finite self-test of Theorem L1 and Lemmas L2, L3 of main text 5.1.
Standard library only. Run: python3 lp_exact.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True

def _norm(c):
    return {k: v for k, v in c.items() if v != 0}

def check_point(cons, x):
    for coef, const, strict in cons:
        val = sum(v * x.get(k, F(0)) for k, v in coef.items()) + const
        if (strict and not val < 0) or (not strict and not val <= 0):
            return False
    return True

def check_cert(cons, lam):
    if any(l < 0 for l in lam.values()):
        return False
    tot = {}
    s = F(0)
    strict_pos = False
    for i, l in lam.items():
        if l == 0:
            continue
        coef, const, strict = cons[i]
        for k, v in coef.items():
            tot[k] = tot.get(k, F(0)) + l * v
        s += l * const
        strict_pos = strict_pos or strict
    if any(v != 0 for v in tot.values()):
        return False
    return s > 0 or (s == 0 and strict_pos)

def feasible(cons):
    """Fourier–Motzkin. Each derived constraint carries its nonnegative multipliers with respect to the original constraints."""
    rows = []
    for i, (coef, const, strict) in enumerate(cons):
        rows.append((_norm(dict(coef)), F(const), bool(strict), {i: F(1)}))
    order = []
    for r in rows:
        for k in r[0]:
            if k not in order:
                order.append(k)
    stages = []                  # row set before each elimination, used for back-substitution
    for var in order:
        stages.append((var, rows))
        pos, neg, zero = [], [], []
        for r in rows:
            a = r[0].get(var, F(0))
            (pos if a > 0 else neg if a < 0 else zero).append(r)
        new = list(zero)
        seen = set()
        for rp in pos:
            ap = rp[0][var]
            for rn in neg:
                an = -rn[0][var]
                # an·rp + ap·rn eliminates var
                coef = {}
                for k, v in rp[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + an * v
                for k, v in rn[0].items():
                    coef[k] = coef.get(k, F(0)) + ap * v
                coef = _norm(coef)
                coef.pop(var, None)
                const = an * rp[1] + ap * rn[1]
                strict = rp[2] or rn[2]
                lam = {}
                for i, l in rp[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + an * l
                for i, l in rn[3].items():
                    lam[i] = lam.get(i, F(0)) + ap * l
                key = (tuple(sorted(coef.items())), const, strict)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                new.append((coef, const, strict, lam))
        rows = new
        for r in rows:          # early stop: a constant contradiction has appeared
            if not r[0] and (r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2])):
                return False, r[3]
    for r in rows:
        if r[1] > 0 or (r[1] == 0 and r[2]):
            return False, r[3]
    # back-substitution for a witness point
    x = {}
    for var, rws in reversed(stages):
        lo, lo_s, hi, hi_s = None, False, None, False
        for coef, const, strict, _ in rws:
            a = coef.get(var, F(0))
            if a == 0:
                continue
            rest = sum(v * x[k] for k, v in coef.items() if k != var) + const
            bnd = -rest / a          # a·var + rest (<,≤) 0
            if a > 0:                # var (<,≤) bnd
                if hi is None or bnd < hi or (bnd == hi and strict):
                    hi, hi_s = bnd, strict if (hi is None or bnd < hi) else (hi_s or strict)
            else:                    # var (>,≥) bnd
                if lo is None or bnd > lo or (bnd == lo and strict):
                    lo, lo_s = bnd, strict if (lo is None or bnd > lo) else (lo_s or strict)
        if lo is None and hi is None:
            val = F(0)
        elif lo is None:
            val = hi - 1 if hi_s else hi
        elif hi is None:
            val = lo + 1 if lo_s else lo
        else:
            val = (lo + hi) / 2 if (lo_s or hi_s) else lo
        x[var] = val
    return True, x

def selftest(n=4000, seed=20261102):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    st = dict(feas=0, infeas=0, bad=0)
    for _ in range(n):
        d = rng.randint(1, 4)
        vs = [f'x{i}' for i in range(d)]
        m = rng.randint(1, 9)
        cons = []
        for _m in range(m):
            coef = {v: F(rng.randint(-4, 4), rng.randint(1, 3)) for v in vs if rng.random() < 0.8}
            cons.append((coef, F(rng.randint(-6, 6), rng.randint(1, 4)), rng.random() < 0.5))
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        if ok:
            st['feas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_point(cons, obj)
        else:
            st['infeas'] += 1
            st['bad'] += not check_cert(cons, obj)
    # boundary cases: x ≤ 0 and x ≥ 0 feasible; x < 0 and x ≥ 0 infeasible; x < 1 and x > 0 feasible (midpoint taken)
    edge = [([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), False), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], True),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(0), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), False)], False),
            ([({'x': F(1)}, F(-1), True), ({'x': F(-1)}, F(0), True)], True),
            ([({}, F(0), True)], False), ([({}, F(0), False)], True)]
    for cons, exp in edge:
        ok, obj = feasible(cons)
        st['bad'] += (ok != exp) or (ok and not check_point(cons, obj)) or ((not ok) and not check_cert(cons, obj))
    return st

if __name__ == '__main__':
    st = selftest()
    print('Exact rational Fourier–Motzkin self-test (4000 random systems, dimension 1–4, strict and non-strict mixed; plus 5 boundary cases)')
    print(f'  feasible {st["feas"]} (witness point substituted and checked constraint by constraint), infeasible {st["infeas"]} (Motzkin certificate checked independently), check failures {st["bad"]}')
~~~

A.5  gate\_criteria.py  Declared instances, runs and settings (verbatim identical to Appendix A.4 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0)

~~~python
"""Basic evaluation library for the gating class: the declared instance Instance (formation record, available positions), legitimate setting runs, the actual event graph and slot reachability, and evaluators for the Chapter 3 verdict path and the F-encoding readings (for the comparisons in main text 3.6 and 7.5).
Library module, not run on its own. Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic.
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
EPS = F(1, 100)
TPOS = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]
NONE = ('none', (0, 0))
SAT, COV, PROF, UNDEF, RDEF = '⊥_Sat', '⊥_Cov', '⊥_Prof', '⊥_Undef', '⊥_Rdef'

def fr(q):
    return f'{q}' if q.denominator < 10 ** 6 else f'{float(q):.6f}'

def k_and(*vals):
    if 0 in vals: return 0
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 1

def k_or(vals):
    vals = list(vals)
    if 1 in vals: return 1
    if any(isinstance(v, str) for v in vals): return '⊥'
    return 0

# ---------------- runs and interventions ----------------
def run_with(G, o0, hist, t_start=0, interv=None, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """Runs hist starting at beat t_start; before each beat executes, interv(t, o) returns the (possibly set) pre-beat state."""
    pre, evs, gates = {}, {}, {}
    o = o0
    for n, sig in enumerate(hist):
        t = t_start + n
        if interv is not None:
            o = interv(t, o)
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t, plastic, bap)
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig, plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        pre[t], evs[t], gates[t] = o, e, rec['g']
        o = o2
    pre[t_start + len(hist)] = o
    return pre, evs, gates

def setw(o, assign):
    w = [r[:] for r in o[0]]
    for (_, i, j), val in assign.items():
        w[i][j] = val
    return (w, o[1], o[2], o[3])

def zk(G, o, k):
    return o[0][k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]

class Instance:
    """Declared instance: G, history, window W = [t0, len(hist)−1], T, protocol switches. The entry beat t0 − 1 lies within the history."""
    def __init__(self, name, G, hist, t0, T=TPOS, plastic=True, bap=True, o0=None):
        self.name, self.G, self.hist, self.t0, self.T = name, G, hist, t0, T
        self.t1 = len(hist) - 1
        self.kw = dict(plastic=plastic, bap=bap)
        self.o0 = o0 or G.init()
        self.pre, self.evs, self.gates = run_with(G, self.o0, hist, **self.kw)
        self.form = {k: [] for k in T}     # D16 (threshold crossing component by component); reused by the R layer
        for t in range(len(hist)):
            for e in self.evs[t].values():
                k = e['tgt']
                if k in T and abs(e['c']) > EPS and zk(G, self.pre[t], k) <= EPS < zk(G, self.pre[t], k) + e['c']:
                    self.form[k].append(t)
        self.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(hist) + 1)
                          if zk(G, self.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in self.form[k])] for k in T}
        self._cf = None
        self._R = None
    def is_avail(self, k, t):
        return t in self.avail[k]
    def Wevents(self):
        return [e for t in range(self.t0, self.t1 + 1) for e in self.evs[t].values()]
    # ---- legitimate setting runs (candidates for positive determination) and envelope pinning (negative determination) ----
    def cf(self):
        if self._cf is not None:
            return self._cf
        G, T = self.G, self.T
        runs = []
        av_any = [t for k in T for t in self.avail[k] if t <= self.t1]
        if not av_any:
            self._cf = (runs, {}, None)
            return self._cf
        first = min(av_any)
        for u in range(first, self.t1 + 1):
            av = [k for k in T if self.is_avail(k, u)]
            if not av:
                continue
            for ks in [[k] for k in av] + ([av] if len(av) > 1 else []):
                for lab in ('b', 'hi'):
                    assign = {k: {'b': G.b, 'hi': G.hi}[lab][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks}
                    if all(assign[k] == self.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]] for k in ks):
                        continue
                    pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, setw(self.pre[u], assign), self.hist[u:], t_start=u, **self.kw)
                    runs.append(dict(u=u, ks=ks, lab=lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        pins = {}
        for lab in ('hi', 'lo'):
            def interv(t, o, lab=lab):
                a = {}
                for k in T:
                    if self.is_avail(k, t):
                        if lab == 'hi':
                            a[k] = G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]
                        elif o[0][k[1]][k[2]] > G.b[k[1]][k[2]]:
                            a[k] = G.b[k[1]][k[2]]
                return setw(o, a) if a else o
            pre, evs, gates = run_with(G, self.pre[first], self.hist[first:], t_start=first, interv=interv, **self.kw)
            flip = [t for t in gates if gates[t] != self.gates[t]]
            pins[lab] = dict(evs=evs, gates=gates, first_flip=min(flip) if flip else None)
            runs.append(dict(u=first, ks=list(T), lab='pin' + lab, evs=evs, gates=gates))
        self._cf = (runs, pins, first)
        return self._cf
    def reach(self):
        if self._R is None:
            srcs = {(k, u) for k in self.T for u in self.avail[k] if u <= self.t1}
            self._R = RW.reach_forward(self.G, self.hist, srcs, **self.kw)
        return self._R

def same_event(evsB, e):
    t, eid = e['t'], e['id']
    if t not in evsB:
        return True
    if eid not in evsB[t]:
        return False
    return evsB[t][eid]['c'] == e['c']

def covered(I, e, first=None):
    """Event kinds covered by Lemma C: their occurrence and component depend only on the gate sequence, the entry letters, x, y and the Structural Positions outside T.
    Lemma C′ (extension): with the gate sequence unchanged, v_j(t) depends on T only through reads, in the beats “after the last reset and before t”, of positions in T pointing to j;
    if no such read occurs in that interval (further truncated to the beats after the first settable beat), LEAK_j@t is covered; RESET_j@t additionally requires that beat t itself has no such read."""
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    Tset = set(I.T)
    tj = {k[2] for k in I.T}
    if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'YIN', 'YDEC', 'XDEC', 'XIN', 'INJ'):
        return True
    if kind in ('SYN', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
        return ('w', idx[0], idx[1]) not in Tset
    if kind in ('LEAK', 'RESET'):
        j = idx[0]
        if j not in tj:
            return True
        if first is None or not hasattr(I, 'gates'):
            return False
        t = e['t']
        last = max([u for u in range(t) if I.gates[u][j]], default=-1)
        lo_t = max(last, first - 1)
        hi_t = t if kind == 'RESET' else t - 1
        for u in range(lo_t + 1, hi_t + 1):
            s = I.G.pat[I.hist[u][0]]
            if any(s[k[1]] for k in I.T if k[2] == j):
                return False
        return True
    return False

def dep(I, e):
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    t = e['t']
    for r in sorted(runs, key=lambda r: -r['u']):
        if r['u'] <= t and not same_event(r['evs'], e):
            return 1, f"LOC beat {r['u']} {','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in r['ks'])}←{r['lab']}"
    if not pins:
        return 0, 'no available position'
    if covered(I, e, first) and all(pins[l]['first_flip'] is None or pins[l]['first_flip'] > t for l in ('hi', 'lo')):
        return 0, 'Lemma C (neither pin flips a gate up to this beat)'
    return COV, 'a pin flips a gate or the kind is not covered by Lemma C; the settings tried did not change the event'

def path(I, e):
    R = I.reach()
    return 1 if (e['reads'] & R[e['t']]) else 0

def eta(I, e):
    """Value and basis of η (or κ). Returns (value, basis, Dep value, Path value, overdetermination flag)."""
    p = path(I, e)
    if p == 0:
        d = (0, 'no route')
        return 0, 'no route (Lemma R3)', 0, 0, False
    dv, why = dep(I, e)
    if dv == 1:
        return 1, why, 1, 1, False
    if dv == 0:
        return 0, 'overdetermined: route but no dependence (' + why + ')', 0, 1, True
    return COV, why, dv, 1, False

def ko_act(G, o, evs, eid, eps=EPS):
    """Lemma A: a as certified by KO. Returns (a, KO difference)."""
    e = evs[eid]; k = e['tgt']
    if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP':
        return None, None
    if k[0] != 'w':
        d = e['c']
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    i, j = k[1], k[2]
    terms = [x['c'] for x in evs.values() if x['tgt'] == k and x['id'][0] != 'CLIP']
    S = o[0][i][j] + sum(terms)
    S_ko = S - e['c']
    clip = lambda z: min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], z))
    inside = lambda z: G.lo[i][j] <= z <= G.hi[i][j]
    d = clip(S) - clip(S_ko)
    if inside(S) and inside(S_ko):
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    return (1 if abs(d) > eps else SAT), d

def evaluate(I, part_all=False):
    """Evaluates the criteria of this paper item by item. With part_all=True, Part computes κ for every B-internal event (for statistics); otherwise it stops at the first witness."""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    rows, hd, hd_cons = [], [], []
    n_over = 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] not in T:
            continue
        a, d = ko_act(G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])
        if a is None:
            a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0   # clipping events are not knockout targets; their action is judged by components (component criterion, General Form line 105)
        ev, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        n_over += over and a == 1
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, d=d, eta=ev, why=why, dep=dv, path=pv, over=over))
        hd.append(k_and(a, ev))
        hd_cons.append(k_and(a, '⊥_Over' if over else ev))
    part, part_cons, wit, nk = [], [], None, 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['car'] != RW.B:
            continue
        kv, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        nk += 1
        part.append(kv); part_cons.append('⊥_Over' if over else kv)
        if kv == 1 and wit is None:
            wit = (e['id'], why)
            if not part_all:
                break
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    q = {k: (int(zk(G, end, k) > EPS), int(bool(I.form[k])), zk(G, end, k)) for k in T}
    qv = k_and(*[k_and(r, f) for (r, f, _) in q.values()])
    trace = k_and(RDEF, qv, 1, 1)      # this evaluator does not contain the R-layer rule for r (r is computed in Appendix A.14); Ident (POS) and HistEv (REC) are 1 by the model structure
    out = dict(rows=rows, HDyn=k_or(hd), HDyn_cons=k_or(hd_cons), Part=k_or(part), Part_cons=k_or(part_cons),
               Part_wit=wit, n_over=n_over, q=q, qv=qv, Trace=trace)
    out['Q'] = k_and(trace, out['Part'], out['HDyn'])
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    out['pins'] = {l: pins[l]['first_flip'] for l in pins} if pins else {}
    out['nruns'] = len(runs)
    out['Wmax_z'] = {k: max(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    out['Wmin_z'] = {k: min(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    return out

# ---------------- verdict path of Chapter 3 on this instance ----------------
def third_path(I, nonF):
    """Computes, level by level under the provisions of *The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field* v1.0, the verdicts that Chapter 3 can give. nonF: the conclusion of nonf_encoding for this host (True means there is no exact F encoding satisfying BEAT and LOC)."""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    allpos = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    steps = []
    steps.append('Y₂: SEP requires “the host is in class F” ([Chapter 3] 184); the mechanism-level declaration entry requires “the law is in class F” (181). '
                 + ('This host has no exact F encoding satisfying BEAT and LOC (Propositions N1, N2), so SEP cannot be declared truthfully and no Y₂ verdict is obtained; as with Instance B: “the feedback law is not among the three numeric laws of class F, so Theorem 6 does not apply (U9). Hence only Y₁ can be used” (639).'
                    if nonF else 'An F encoding exists and Y₂ is available (this branch does not occur for this instance).'))
    steps.append('If the entry is forced with a 488-type encoding (occurrence as actually declared, constant law): EF4 fails (485), so the negative determination of Theorem 7(2) does not hold; the LOC response conflicts with the empty support of the encoding, which is E25 (554). Its F-layer readings are given in the F-encoding column.')
    q = {}
    for k in T:
        q[k] = 0 if zk(G, end, k) <= EPS else PROF
    steps.append('Y₁ (4.5, 241): q(x) is 0 if s(x) ≤ ε at window end, otherwise ⊥_Prof → ' + str({f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}': q[k] for k in T}))
    steps.append('Y₁ (242): r is ⊥_Prof (Theorem 5(2))')
    low = all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in T)
    ph = 0 if low else PROF
    steps.append(f'Y₁ (243): Part and HDyn are 0 when “no position of T ever exceeds ε within W”, otherwise ⊥_Prof → T stays ≤ ε within W: {low} ⇒ Part = HDyn = {ph}')
    steps.append('Y₁ (244): Act(m) and HistCond(m) are ⊥_Undef without an inventory; (245) Fld is ⊥_Prof')
    fld_neg = all(zk(G, end, k) <= EPS for k in allpos) or all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in allpos)
    steps.append('Y₁ plus declaration (247): q = 1 requires “F-class declaration and REC”; no F-class declaration is available, so q stays ⊥_Prof; '
                 f'(248) the degenerate negative determination of Fld requires Dom5 and either no position > ε at window end or every position ≤ ε throughout W: {fld_neg}; '
                 '(249) r requires PE (196); the signal gates in-boundary plasticity through the teaching current, so PE fails; '
                 '(250) Part requires AC (198); output firing is not a pure-read process (firing writes y and gates PLUS), so AC fails; '
                 '(251) HDyn requires SAM and LOC and class F (Lemma 10, 494); class F fails')
    fld = 0 if fld_neg else PROF
    return dict(q=q, r=PROF, Part=ph, HDyn=ph, Fld=fld, steps=steps)

# ---------------- readings of the 488-type F encoding (D10–D19) ----------------
def f_layer(I, positions, ext_entry, eps=EPS):
    """The entry beat t0−1 gives z(t0) through a declared event; every plasticity event in W acting on the position set is encoded at its actual time as a constant-law mechanism (occurrence declared, cert_eff = 1).
    With ext_entry=True the entry events at T positions are recorded as out-of-boundary pulses (E2), otherwise as B-internal constant-law mechanisms (E1). Evaluated by D12, D15–D19 and 4.2."""
    G, T, t0, t1 = I.G, I.T, I.t0, I.t1
    s = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
    form, mark = {k: [] for k in positions}, {k: [] for k in positions}
    dom_err = None
    for k in positions:
        z0 = zk(G, I.pre[t0], k)
        if z0 == 0:
            continue
        if z0 < 0:
            dom_err = dom_err or f'entry-beat position w{k[1]}{k[2]} has z(t0) = {fr(z0)} < 0 and leaves the domain of F (D10, D20)'
            continue
        if ext_entry and k in T:
            if z0 > eps: form[k].append(t0 - 1); mark[k].append(t0 - 1)
        elif z0 > eps:
            form[k].append(t0 - 1)
        s[k] = z0
    devs, atoms = [], []
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        incr = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] in positions and e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
                c = e['c']
                act = 1 if abs(c) > eps else 0          # D18 Act_F
                hc = 0                                  # constant law: Supp_act = ∅ (D15) ⇒ HistCond_F = 0 (note after D18)
                atoms.append(dict(id=e['id'], t=t, tgt=e['tgt'], c=c, Act=act, HistCond=hc, Cond=0))
                if act:
                    if s[e['tgt']] <= eps < s[e['tgt']] + c:
                        form[e['tgt']].append(t)
                    incr[e['tgt']] += c                 # 4.2: only effective components are booked
        for k in positions:
            s[k] += incr[k]
            if s[k] < 0 and dom_err is None:
                dom_err = f'beat {t}, position w{k[1]}{k[2]}: updated value {fr(s[k])} < 0 (D20)'
        for k in positions:
            if s[k] != zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k):
                devs.append((t, k, s[k] - zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k)))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[k] for k in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[k] > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    trace = k_and(Src, Ret, 1, 1)
    part = k_or([a['Cond'] for a in atoms])
    hdyn = k_or([k_and(1 if a['tgt'] in T else 0, a['Act'], a['HistCond']) for a in atoms])
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Trace=trace, Part=part, HDyn=hdyn, Q=k_and(trace, part, hdyn),
                atoms=atoms, devs=devs, dom_err=dom_err)
~~~

A.6  decision.py  Exact 𝒟₁ adjudication and the verdicts for Part and HDyn (verbatim identical to Appendix A.6 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0)

~~~python
"""R-layer adjudicator for the gating class (main text 5.2, 5.3 and Section 6): exact adjudication of functional dependence on 𝒟₁ (Theorem D1), the Part and HDyn verdicts with the three-tier negative (Theorem D2), T_max coverage (Theorem TM).
Declared intervention domain: 𝒟₁ (single-beat joint LOC, σ_k ∈ [b_k, hi_k]); on 𝒟_m (multi-beat) there are only the sufficient negative determinations of Lemmas C and C′.
Clipping individuation: each saturated position–beat pair is evaluated under the two readings “separate” and “joint (D9)”; if they agree, the common value is taken, otherwise ⊥_Ind.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 decision.py (the output contains “time” lines, which vary with the machine; everything else is deterministic)
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import lp_exact as LP
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
# ---------- affine forms: (constant, {variable: coefficient}) ----------

def A0(c):
    return (F(c), {})

def avar(name):
    return (F(0), {name: F(1)})

def aadd(*xs):
    c, d = F(0), {}
    for x0, x1 in xs:
        c += x0
        for k, v in x1.items():
            d[k] = d.get(k, F(0)) + v
    return (c, {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v != 0})

def ascale(a, s):
    return (a[0] * s, {k: v * s for k, v in a[1].items() if v * s != 0})

def is_const(a):
    return not a[1]

def le0(a):      # a ≤ 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], False)

def lt0(a):      # a < 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], True)

class Stats:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lp = 0
        self.nodes = 0

ST = Stats()

def feas(R):
    ST.lp += 1
    return LP.feasible(R)

def simplify(R):
    """Among constraints with the same direction keep only the tightest (coefficient vector normalized by its largest absolute value). An equivalence transformation: the feasible set does not change (Lemma L3 of the main text)."""
    best = {}
    for coef, const, strict in R:
        m = max(abs(v) for v in coef.values())
        key = tuple(sorted((k, v / m) for k, v in coef.items()))
        c = const / m
        old = best.get(key)
        if old is None or c > old[0] or (c == old[0] and strict and not old[1]):
            best[key] = (c, strict)
    return [(dict(k), c, s_) for k, (c, s_) in best.items()]

def split(R, conds):
    """conds: [(label, [constraints])]; returns the feasible (label, R + constraints). Constant constraints are evaluated directly."""
    out = []
    for lab, cs in conds:
        add, ok = [], True
        for c in cs:
            if not c[0]:
                v = c[1]
                if (c[2] and not v < 0) or (not c[2] and not v <= 0):
                    ok = False
                    break
            else:
                add.append(c)
        if not ok:
            continue
        R2 = simplify(R + add) if add else R
        if add:
            f, _ = feas(R2)
            if not f:
                continue
        out.append((lab, R2))
    return out

def sym_step(G, st, R, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """One symbolic beat of Definition 2. st = (w{(i,j): affine}, v[affine], x[const], y[const]). Returns [(R′, st′, events{id: affine c}, net increments{(i,j): affine})]."""
    w, v, x, y = st
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = [aadd(ascale(v[j], G.lam[j]), *[w[(i, j)] for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]], A0(I[j])) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    gate_opts = [[]]
    regs = [R]
    combos = [([], R)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        nxt = []
        for gs, Rg in combos:
            d = aadd(vt[j], A0(-G.theta[j]))      # vt − θ
            for lab, R2 in split(Rg, [(1, [le0(ascale(d, -1))]), (0, [lt0(d)])]):
                nxt.append((gs + [lab], R2))
        combos = nxt
    out = []
    for g, Rg in combos:
        v2 = [A0(G.vreset[j]) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        ev0 = {}
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            ev0[('LEAK', (j,))] = ascale(v[j], G.lam[j] - 1)
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                if s[i]:
                    ev0[('SYN', (i, j))] = w[(i, j)]
            if I[j] != 0:
                ev0[('INJ', (j,))] = A0(I[j])
            if g[j]:
                ev0[('RESET', (j,))] = aadd(A0(G.vreset[j]), ascale(vt[j], -1))
                ev0[('YIN', (j,))] = A0(1)
            ev0[('YDEC', (j,))] = A0((G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            ev0[('XDEC', (i,))] = A0((G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
            if s[i]:
                ev0[('XIN', (i,))] = A0(1)
        Ssum = {}
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                parts = [w[(i, j)]]
                if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                    c = A0(G.Ap * x2[i]); ev0[('PLUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                    c = A0(-G.Am * y2[j]); ev0[('MINUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    c = ascale(aadd(w[(i, j)], A0(-G.b[i][j])), -G.rho); ev0[('RELAX', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                Ssum[(i, j)] = aadd(*parts)
        pieces = [({}, Rg)]
        for k, S in Ssum.items():
            lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
            nxt = []
            for asg, Rp in pieces:
                opts = [('lo', [lt0(aadd(S, A0(-lo)))]),                                       # S < lo
                        ('mid', [le0(aadd(A0(lo), ascale(S, -1))), le0(aadd(S, A0(-hi)))]),     # lo ≤ S ≤ hi
                        ('hi', [lt0(aadd(A0(hi), ascale(S, -1)))])]                              # S > hi
                for lab, R2 in split(Rp, opts):
                    a2 = dict(asg); a2[k] = lab
                    nxt.append((a2, R2))
            pieces = nxt
        for asg, Rp in pieces:
            ev = dict(ev0)
            w2, net = {}, {}
            for k, S in Ssum.items():
                lab = asg[k]
                lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
                if lab == 'mid':
                    w2[k] = S
                else:
                    b = A0(lo if lab == 'lo' else hi)
                    w2[k] = b
                    ev[('CLIP', k)] = aadd(b, ascale(S, -1))
                net[k] = aadd(w2[k], ascale(w[k], -1))
            out.append((Rp, (w2, v2, x2, y2), ev, net))
    return out

def point_of(R, names):
    ok, x = feas(R)
    assert ok
    return {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}

def differs(R, a, c0):
    """Whether R contains a point at which the affine form a ≠ c0; returns a witness point or None."""
    if is_const(a):
        if a[0] != c0:
            ok, x = feas(R) if R else (True, {})
            return x
        return None
    for cons in (lt0(aadd(a, A0(-c0))), lt0(aadd(A0(c0), ascale(a, -1)))):
        ok, x = feas(simplify(R + [cons]))
        if ok:
            return x
    return None

# ---------- instances and exact adjudication ----------
class Decider:
    def __init__(self, I, budget=200000):
        self.I, self.G = I, I.G
        self.budget = budget
        self.sat = {}            # (k, t) saturated T position–beat → actual net increment
        for t in range(len(I.hist)):
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in I.T:
                    k = e['tgt']
                    self.sat[(k, t)] = I.pre[t + 1][0][k[1]][k[2]] - I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]
        self.fd1 = {}            # event id → (u, witness dict); records only those judged 1
        self.fd1_net = {}
        self.complete_u = {}     # u → whether exhausted within the budget
        self.verify_fail = 0
        self._run()
    def avail_T(self, u):
        return [k for k in self.I.T if self.I.is_avail(k, u)]
    def targets(self):
        I = self.I
        return {e['id'] for e in I.Wevents()}
    def _run(self):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        tgt = self.targets()
        us = [u for u in range(0, I.t1 + 1) if self.avail_T(u)]
        for u in us:
            S = self.avail_T(u)
            names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
            w0 = {(i, j): A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
            R0 = []
            for k, nm in zip(S, names):
                w0[(k[1], k[2])] = avar(nm)
                R0.append(le0(aadd(A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), ascale(avar(nm), -1))))      # b − σ ≤ 0
                R0.append(le0(aadd(avar(nm), A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))               # σ − hi ≤ 0
            st0 = (w0, [A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
            nodes = 0
            stack = [(u, st0, R0)]
            complete = True
            while stack:
                t, st, R = stack.pop()
                nodes += 1
                if nodes > self.budget:
                    complete = False
                    break
                for R2, st2, ev, net in sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw):
                    for eid_s, e in I.evs[t].items():
                        if eid_s not in tgt or eid_s in self.fd1:
                            continue
                        key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        if key not in ev:
                            wit = point_of(R2, names)
                        else:
                            x = differs(R2, ev[key], e['c'])
                            wit = None if x is None else {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}
                        if wit is not None:
                            self._verify(u, S, names, wit, e)
                            self.fd1[eid_s] = (u, {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    for (k, tt), net0 in self.sat.items():
                        if tt == t and (k, t) not in self.fd1_net and t >= I.t0:
                            x = differs(R2, net[(k[1], k[2])], net0)
                            if x is not None:
                                self.fd1_net[(k, t)] = (u, {kk: x.get(nm, F(0)) for kk, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    if t + 1 <= I.t1:
                        stack.append((t + 1, st2, R2))
            ST.nodes += nodes
            self.complete_u[u] = complete
    def _verify(self, u, S, names, wit, e):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        assign = {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)}
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], assign), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        if GK.same_event(evs, e):
            self.verify_fail += 1
    def fd1_value(self, e):
        if e['id'] in self.fd1:
            return 1
        us = [u for u in self.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
        return 0 if all(self.complete_u[u] for u in us) else GK.COV

def path_act(I, sources, e):
    """Whether e is reachable from sources = {(node, beat)} on the actual event graph (D13: actually occurring events only; read edges, write edges, Structural-Position persistence edges)."""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == e['t']:
            return 1 if (e['reads'] & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            if ev['reads'] & reach:
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """Returns a dict: value, tier, basis. domain='D1' uses exact adjudication on 𝒟₁; 'Dm' also uses Lemmas C, C′ and pin witnesses."""
    if GK.path(I, e) == 0:
        return dict(v=0, tier='a', why='Path_slot = 0 (R3)', fd=0)
    fd = D.fd1_value(e)
    wit = D.fd1.get(e['id'])
    if domain == 'Dm' and fd != 1:
        dv, why = GK.dep(I, e)
        if dv == 1:
            fd, wit = 1, None
        elif dv == 0 and fd == 0:
            fd = 0
        else:
            fd = GK.COV
    if fd == 1:
        if wit is not None:
            u, asg = wit
            src = {(k, u) for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            why = f'𝒟₁ witness at beat {u}: ' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}←{fr(val)}' for k, val in asg.items())
        else:
            src = {(k, u) for k in I.T for u in I.avail[k] if u <= e['t']}
            why = 'pin witness (𝒟_m)'
        if path_act(I, src, e):
            return dict(v=1, tier='+', why=why + '; reachable in the actual graph', fd=1)
        return dict(v='⊥_Open', tier='+?', why=why + '; not reachable in the actual graph (the dependence runs only through branches that did not occur)', fd=1)
    if fd == 0:
        return dict(v=0 if bfd else '⊥_Over', tier='b', why=f'FD_{domain} = 0 (all branches infeasible or constants equal)' + ('' if bfd else '; B_FD not declared'), fd=0)
    return dict(v=GK.COV, tier='c', why='not exhausted within budget', fd=GK.COV)

def judge(I, D, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """Part and HDyn verdicts (separate reading and joint reading; if the two agree, the common value is taken, otherwise ⊥_Ind)."""
    T = I.T
    rows = []
    hd_sep, hd_joint, pt_sep, pt_joint = [], [], [], []
    satk = {(k, t) for (k, t) in D.sat if t >= I.t0}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        ex = eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd, domain)
        a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
        ko = GK.ko_act(I.G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])[0]
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, ko=ko, **ex))
        if e['tgt'] in T:
            hd_sep.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
            if (e['tgt'], e['t']) not in satk:
                hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
        if e['car'] == RW.B:
            pt_sep.append(ex['v'])
            if not (e['tgt'] in T and (e['tgt'], e['t']) in satk):
                pt_joint.append(ex['v'])
    for (k, t) in satk:      # joint event: c = net increment, reads = union over the members
        net0 = D.sat[(k, t)]
        mem = [e for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] == k]
        reads = frozenset().union(*[e['reads'] for e in mem])
        pseudo = dict(id=('NET', (k[1], k[2]), t), t=t, reads=reads, tgt=k, car=RW.B, c=net0)
        if net0 == 0:
            a = '⊥_Joint' if any(e['c'] != 0 for e in mem) else 0
        else:
            a = 1 if abs(net0) > EPS else 0
        if GK.path(I, pseudo) == 0:
            ev = 0
        elif (k, t) in D.fd1_net:
            u, asg = D.fd1_net[(k, t)]
            src = {(kk, u) for kk, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][kk[1]][kk[2]]}
            ev = 1 if path_act(I, src, pseudo) else '⊥_Open'
        else:
            ev = 0 if bfd else '⊥_Over'
        hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ev)); pt_joint.append(ev)
    hs, hj, ps, pj = GK.k_or(hd_sep), GK.k_or(hd_joint), GK.k_or(pt_sep), GK.k_or(pt_joint)
    H = hs if hs == hj else '⊥_Ind'
    P = ps if ps == pj else '⊥_Ind'
    return dict(rows=rows, HDyn=H, Part=P, HDyn_sep=hs, HDyn_joint=hj, nsat=len(satk))

def premises(I, D, res, bfd):
    """Antecedent list for a verdict of 0 (counterparts of EF1–EF5, main text 5.3)."""
    base = ['MOD-R (R1: event inventory complete, model structure)', 'R2/R3 (read sets and slot graph closed, model structure)', 'REC', 'ENT', 'POS', 'BEAT-R (model beat is the L beat, ε declared)']
    tiers = {r['tier'] for r in res['rows'] if r['v'] == 0}
    if 'b' in tiers:
        base += ['DOM[𝒟₁] (intervention domain declared in advance)', 'B_FD[𝒟₁] (object-level correspondence of the functional-dependence reading)']
    if res['nsat']:
        base += ['IND_CLIP (clipping individuation; dispensable only when the two readings agree)']
    return base

# ---------- report ----------
def show(I, title, bfd=True, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.time()
    D = Decider(I)
    res = judge(I, D, bfd)
    resn = judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'[{title}] {I.name}')
    print(f'  W = beats {I.t0}–{I.t1}; first available beat of T: {min(min(I.avail[k]) for k in I.T if I.avail[k])}; '
          f'𝒟₁ start beats: {len(D.complete_u)}, all exhausted within budget: {all(D.complete_u.values())}; concrete witness re-run check failures: {D.verify_fail}; saturated position–beat pairs: {len(D.sat)}')
    if verbose:
        print('  In-W events whose action target is in T: event@beat | c | a (read-out/KO) | η | tier | basis')
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in I.T:
                print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {r["a"]}/{r["ko"]} | {r["v"]} | {r["tier"]} | {r["why"]}')
    cnt = {}
    for r in res['rows']:
        if r['e']['car'] == RW.B:
            cnt[(r['tier'], str(r['v']))] = cnt.get((r['tier'], str(r['v'])), 0) + 1
    print('  Tier distribution of κ over in-B events:', '; '.join(f'{t}:{v}×{n}' for (t, v), n in sorted(cnt.items())))
    print(f'  Verdict (B_FD[𝒟₁] declared): Part = {res["Part"]}, HDyn = {res["HDyn"]} (separate {res["HDyn_sep"]} / joint {res["HDyn_joint"]})')
    print(f'  Verdict (B_FD not declared): Part = {resn["Part"]}, HDyn = {resn["HDyn"]}')
    if res['HDyn'] == 0 or res['Part'] == 0:
        print('  Antecedents of the 0 verdict:', '; '.join(premises(I, D, res, bfd)))
    print(f'  (time {time.time() - t0:.1f} s)')
    return D, res, resn

def random_validation(n_inst=40, seed=7, samples=60):
    """Finite verification of Theorem D1: random K_g members and histories; for every event judged FD₁ = 0, settings sampled at random within 𝒟₁ all leave that event unchanged;
    for every event judged 1 the witness has been re-run concretely. Also compared with the finite-setting criterion of gate_criteria (judged 1 there ⇒ judged 1 here)."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(inst=0, events=0, fd0=0, fd1=0, samp=0, viol=0, vfail=0, gk1_not1=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['hi'] = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), 2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        form = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 3)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(3, 7))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
        st['inst'] += 1; st['vfail'] += D.verify_fail
        for e in I.Wevents():
            v = D.fd1_value(e)
            st['events'] += 1
            if v == 1:
                st['fd1'] += 1
            elif v == 0:
                st['fd0'] += 1
                us = [u for u in D.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
                for _s in range(samples // 10 if us else 0):
                    u = rng.choice(us)
                    asg = {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 48), 48) * (G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]) for k in D.avail_T(u)}
                    _, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
                    st['samp'] += 1
                    if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                        st['viol'] += 1
            dv, _ = GK.dep(I, e)
            if dv == 1 and v != 1:
                # the pin witnesses of gate_criteria belong to 𝒟_m and may lie outside 𝒟₁; count only cases where its single-beat witness is not judged 1 by 𝒟₁
                runs, _, _ = I.cf()
                single = any((not r['lab'].startswith('pin')) and r['u'] <= e['t'] and not GK.same_event(r['evs'], e) for r in runs)
                st['gk1_not1'] += single
    return st

def tmax_coverage():
    """Verdict coverage of the T_max reduction (Theorem TM of the main text): the formative history makes all 4 entry synapses Trace-Responses, T_max = 4 positions;
    Part and HDyn are evaluated for every nonempty subset of T_max and monotonicity is checked (T ⊆ T′ ⇒ the verdict does not decrease in the truth order, and 1 and 0 do not flip)."""
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    hist = [('AB', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0)), NONE, ('B', (F(1, 2), 0)), NONE, ('A', (F(1, 4), 0))]
    allT = [('w', i, 0) for i in range(4)]
    base = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=allT)
    end = base.pre[len(hist)]
    Tmax = [k for k in allT if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and base.form[k]]
    print(f'  history (AB+teach 1, rest, rest, A, rest, B+teach 1/2, rest, A+teach 1/4), W = beats 3–7; T_max = {[f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Tmax]}')
    res = {}
    for r in range(1, len(Tmax) + 1):
        for Ts in itertools.combinations(Tmax, r):
            I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=list(Ts))
            D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
            j = judge(I, D, True)
            res[Ts] = (j['Part'], j['HDyn'], all(D.complete_u.values()), D.verify_fail)
    order = {0: 0, '⊥': 1, 1: 2}
    viol = 0
    for Ta in res:
        for Tb in res:
            if set(Ta) < set(Tb):
                for idx in (0, 1):
                    va, vb = res[Ta][idx], res[Tb][idx]
                    if va in (0, 1) and vb in (0, 1) and va > vb:
                        viol += 1
                    if va == 1 and vb != 1:
                        viol += 1
                    if vb == 0 and va != 0:
                        viol += 1
    for Ts, v in sorted(res.items(), key=lambda kv: len(kv[0])):
        print(f'    T = {{{",".join(f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Ts)}}}: Part = {v[0]}, HDyn = {v[1]}, exhausted {v[2]}, witness check failures {v[3]}')
    print(f'  monotonicity violations over subset pairs T ⊂ T′: {viol} (1 stays 1 under enlargement, 0 stays 0 under shrinking)')
    return viol

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 frozen declared history', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+teach 1, B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 declared history, W = (A+teach 1, rest, rest)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('formation once; entry beat A+teach; W = (B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2, A₊ = 1/4; formation (A+teach, rest, rest)×6, rest 8, W is (A,rest,rest,B,rest,rest)×3', Gs,
                      FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    print('1. Instance verdicts (exact adjudication on 𝒟₁; three-tier negative for HistCond; separate/joint readings)')
    for I, t in ((P, 'Instance P'), (N, 'Instance N'), (O1, 'Instance O1'), (O2, 'Instance O2'), (SAT, 'Instance SAT (clipping in effect)')):
        show(I, t, verbose=(I is not SAT))
        print()
    print('2. Finite verification of Theorem D1 (random K_g members and histories; events with FD₁ = 0 stay unchanged under random sampling within 𝒟₁; witnesses for verdict 1 have been concretely re-run)')
    st = random_validation()
    print(f'  {st["inst"]} instances, {st["events"]} events in W: FD₁ = 1 for {st["fd1"]}, = 0 for {st["fd0"]}; {st["samp"]} samples, {st["viol"]} violations; '
          f'{st["vfail"]} witness re-run failures; single-beat witnesses of the finite-setting criterion not judged 1 by 𝒟₁: {st["gk1_not1"]}')
    print('3. Verdict coverage of the T_max reduction')
    tmax_coverage()
    print(f'4. Linear-feasibility calls in total: {ST.lp}; symbolic branch nodes in total: {ST.nodes}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.7  common12.py  The m family and reading conventions (verbatim identical to Appendix A.4 of *The Mathematical Form of Sedimentation-Dynamics Concretization* v1.0)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Sedimentation-Dynamics Concretization: common definitions (standard library only; exact rational arithmetic).
This module implements the host-independent parts:
  validity conditions for the common-level function m (The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.2, Appendix 1, Section 5, fragment B5: declared in advance, idempotent m(c,…,c) = c, intermediate, symmetric)
  the m_α family: m_α(δ_K) = min + α(max − min), α ∈ [0, 1] ∩ ℚ (Lemma M1: the range of c* over valid m is exactly [min, max] ∩ ℚ)
  three kinds of relative reading: first-opened set Y_first, order of precedence Y_order, share Y_share (fragment B4: "the reading may be the first-opened set, the order of precedence or shares")
  member precedence Lead_q (sole lead / tied lead / not leading; original names in The General Form of Sedimentation Dynamics v1.2, Appendix 1, Section 5)
  evaluation of B_m and B_m^q (fragment B5); an undefined reading is recorded as 'undef-reading' and an undefined I_m as 'undef-Im'; neither is judged false
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import itertools
INF = None   # a time with no opening is recorded as None (i.e. +∞)

# ---------- common-level function m ----------
def m_min(ds):
    return min(ds)

def m_max(ds):
    return max(ds)

def m_mean(ds):
    return sum(ds, F(0)) / len(ds)

def m_alpha(alpha):
    alpha = F(alpha)
    assert 0 <= alpha <= 1
    def m(ds):
        return min(ds) + alpha * (max(ds) - min(ds))
    m.__name__ = f'm_alpha({alpha})'
    return m

M_STD = {'min': m_min, 'mean': m_mean, 'max': m_max}

def check_m_legal(m, samples):
    """The three machine-checkable validity conditions out of four: idempotent, intermediate, symmetric (declaration in advance is guaranteed by the procedure). samples is a list of Sedimentation-Degree tuples."""
    for ds in samples:
        ds = [F(d) for d in ds]
        c = ds[0]
        if m([c] * len(ds)) != c:
            return False, ('idempotent', ds)
        v = m(ds)
        if not (min(ds) <= v <= max(ds)):
            return False, ('intermediate', ds)
        for p in itertools.permutations(ds):
            if m(list(p)) != v:
                return False, ('symmetric', ds)
    return True, None

# ---------- readings ----------
def y_first(times):
    """times: {path: first opening beat or None}. Returns the first-opened set (the empty set if nothing opens within W)."""
    fin = [t for t in times.values() if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset()
    t0 = min(fin)
    return frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == t0)

def y_order(times):
    """Order of precedence: the paths that open within W in the weak order of their first opening beats (ties grouped); paths that never open are listed separately. Reads only the relative order, not absolute beats."""
    fin = sorted(set(t for t in times.values() if t is not None))
    groups = tuple(frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == tt) for tt in fin)
    never = frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t is None)
    return (groups, never)

def y_share(counts):
    """Share: counts {path: throughput}; the reading is undefined when the total is 0 (as in the domain condition of FlowShare in Field Chapter 11; fragment C2)."""
    tot = sum(counts.values(), F(0))
    if tot == 0:
        return 'undef'
    return tuple(sorted((p, F(c) / tot) for p, c in counts.items()))

def lead(reading_kind, Y, q):
    """Member precedence Lead_q ∈ {sole lead, tied lead, not leading}; returns 'undef' when the share reading is undefined."""
    if reading_kind == 'first':
        if Y == frozenset([q]):
            return 'sole lead'
        return 'tied lead' if q in Y else 'not leading'
    if reading_kind == 'order':
        groups, never = Y
        if not groups or q not in groups[0]:
            return 'not leading'
        return 'sole lead' if len(groups[0]) == 1 else 'tied lead'
    if reading_kind == 'share':
        if Y == 'undef':
            return 'undef'
        d = dict(Y)
        mx = max(d.values())
        tops = [p for p, v in d.items() if v == mx]
        if q not in tops:
            return 'not leading'
        return 'sole lead' if len(tops) == 1 else 'tied lead'
    raise ValueError(reading_kind)

def B_value(Y0, Y1):
    """Value of B_m: called when I_m is defined. If the reading on either side is undefined, records 'undef-reading' (not judged false)."""
    if Y0 == 'undef' or Y1 == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return Y0 != Y1

def Bq_value(kind, Y0, Y1, q):
    a, b = lead(kind, Y0, q), lead(kind, Y1, q)
    if a == 'undef' or b == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return a != b

def fmt(x):
    if isinstance(x, F):
        return str(x)
    if isinstance(x, frozenset):
        return '{' + ','.join(str(e) for e in sorted(x, key=str)) + '}'
    if isinstance(x, tuple):
        return '(' + ', '.join(fmt(e) for e in x) + ')'
    return str(x)
~~~

A.8  fam\_jia\_wu.py  Physical mechanism models of Families A and E, and I\_m (verbatim identical to Appendix A.6 of *The Mathematical Form of Sedimentation-Dynamics Concretization* v1.0)

~~~python
"""Hosts B and C: I_m, B_m (share reading), F, Ω_W and Bias on Family A (constant-head two-channel flow network) and Family E (firing-rate neural network) of the Construction Form.
Run: python3 fam_jia_wu.py
The physical mechanism models are rewritten term by term from the constitutive antecedents and parameters of The Construction Form of Field Concretization v1.0, 4.2 and 4.3 (Sections 4.2 and 4.3 of that paper), in exact rational arithmetic:
  Family A: Qᵢ = (g₀ + κ_cons zᵢ)ΔP; write φcQᵢΔt/ℓ; relaxation −γzᵢΔt goes into the store Lᵢ; store output min(Lᵢ, eQᵢΔt) (store at the start of the beat); seed dumps 2 A_u into channel 1.
  Family E: yᵢ = β(w₀ + zᵢ)x; write ηxyᵢΔt; decay −γzᵢΔt; receptor pool Rᵢ −= (write + induction − decay)/g₁; pair induces +2 S_u at synapse 1.
Notation: the conductance coefficient κ of C1 in The Construction Form of Field Concretization and the continuation tendency κ of Field Chapter 1, Definition 11 share a name but not a meaning; this paper writes the former as κ_cons.
Declared augmentation (marked "aug", not part of the original declaration of The Construction Form of Field Concretization): Family A⁺ adds a frozen blockage flag brkᵢ (when brkᵢ = 1, gᵢ = 0 and the channel does not conduct).
Reading: share Y_share = the ratio of ΣQᵢ (Family A) or Σyᵢ (Family E) within W; neither family has an outflow threshold, so the first-opened and order-of-precedence readings have no object (Field Chapter 1, Equation 4 and Theorem 17 have no direct object).
I_m (path-level model (A), path = channel / synapse, Depth = zᵢ): z₁ = z₂ = c*; Lᵢ, brk and the input protocol are frozen; the receptor pool of Family E follows, recomputed by N5 conservation (write-back rule R-pool, see 10.4 of the main text).
"""
import sys, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha, fmt
# ---------------- Family A ----------------
JIA = dict(g0=F(1), kap=F(1), c=F(1, 5), phi=F(1, 2), ell=F(1), gam=F(1, 10), e=F(1, 5), zmax=F(10))

def jia_step(st, letter, P=JIA, brk=(0, 0)):
    z, L = list(st['z']), list(st['L'])
    dP = F(1) if letter == 'flow' else F(0)
    Q = [(0 if brk[i] else (P['g0'] + P['kap'] * z[i])) * dP for i in range(2)]
    exp_ = [min(L[i], P['e'] * Q[i]) for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['phi'] * P['c'] * Q[i] / P['ell'] for i in range(2)]
    rel = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - rel[i] for i in range(2)]
    L2 = [L[i] + rel[i] * P['ell'] - exp_[i] for i in range(2)]
    if letter == 'seed':
        z2[0] += 2
    assert all(0 <= v <= P['zmax'] for v in z2) and all(v >= 0 for v in L2), 'outside the domain of validity'
    return dict(z=z2, L=L2), Q

def jia_run(st, hist, P=JIA, brk_sched=None):
    Qs = []
    for t, a in enumerate(hist):
        brk = brk_sched[t] if brk_sched else (0, 0)
        st, Q = jia_step(st, a, P, brk)
        Qs.append(Q)
    return st, Qs

# ---------------- Family E ----------------
WU = dict(w0=F(1), beta=F(1), eta=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), g1=F(1, 10), ymax=F(100))

def wu_step(st, letter, P=WU):
    z, R = list(st['z']), list(st['R'])
    x = F(1) if letter == 'drive' else F(0)
    y = [P['beta'] * (P['w0'] + z[i]) * x for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['eta'] * x * y[i] for i in range(2)]
    dec = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    ind = [F(2) if (letter == 'pair' and i == 0) else F(0) for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - dec[i] + ind[i] for i in range(2)]
    R2 = [R[i] - (wr[i] + ind[i] - dec[i]) / P['g1'] for i in range(2)]
    phys = all(v >= 0 for v in R2) and all(v <= P['ymax'] for v in y) and all(v >= 0 for v in z2)
    return dict(z=z2, R=R2), y, phys

def wu_run(st, hist, P=WU):
    ys, ok = [], True
    for a in hist:
        st, y, phys = wu_step(st, a, P)
        ys.append(y); ok = ok and phys
    return st, ys, ok

# ---------------- readings and I_m ----------------
def share_of(series, beats=None):
    beats = range(len(series)) if beats is None else beats
    return y_share({i + 1: sum((series[t][i] for t in beats), F(0)) for i in range(2)})

def Im_jia(st, m):
    c = m(st['z'])
    ext = sum((c - zi) * JIA['ell'] for zi in st['z'])       # volume of structural material exchanged with outside B_phys (positive = added)
    return dict(z=[c, c], L=list(st['L'])), c, ext

def Im_wu(st, m, rule='pool'):
    """rule='pool': the receptor pool follows by N5 conservation (R is a quantity determined by Sedimentation-Degree); rule='frozen': the pool is frozen (recorded as physically invalid when N5 is violated)."""
    c = m(st['z'])
    if rule == 'pool':
        R = [st['R'][i] - (c - st['z'][i]) / WU['g1'] for i in range(2)]
        feasible = all(r >= 0 for r in R)
    else:
        R = list(st['R'])
        feasible = all(c == zi for zi in st['z'])               # a frozen pool with changed conductance violates receptor conservation N5
    return dict(z=[c, c], R=R), c, feasible

MFAM = [m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4)), m_alpha(F(3, 4))]

def thmJ1_check():
    """Finite check of Theorems J-1 / W-1: Family A (κ_cons ≥ 0, 0 ≤ γ ≤ 1, W contains only flow, rest) and Family E (W contains only drive, rest):
       the share reading is defined ⇔ W contains a conducting beat; when defined, B_m ⇔ κ_eff > 0 ∧ ∃ conducting beat t with d(t) ≠ 0; independent of m; after I_m, z₁ ≡ z₂."""
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n = viol = 0
    zs = [F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2), F(5)]
    for kap in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for gam in (F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1)):
            P = dict(JIA, kap=kap, gam=gam)
            for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
                for _ in range(6):
                    L = rng.randint(1, 6)
                    hist = [rng.choice(['flow', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
                    st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P)
                    Y0 = share_of(Q0)
                    # prediction
                    d = z1 - z2
                    pred_true = False
                    for a in hist:
                        if a == 'flow':
                            if kap > 0 and d != 0:
                                pred_true = True
                            d = d * (1 + P['phi'] * P['c'] * kap - gam)
                        else:
                            d = d * (1 - gam)
                    defined = 'flow' in hist
                    for m in MFAM:
                        c = m([z1, z2])
                        st1 = dict(z=[c, c], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                        st_end, Q1 = jia_run(st1, hist, P)
                        Y1 = share_of(Q1)
                        B = B_value(Y0, Y1)
                        n += 1
                        if not defined:
                            viol += (B != 'undef-reading')
                        else:
                            viol += (B != pred_true)
                        viol += (st_end['z'][0] != st_end['z'][1])
    nw = vw = 0
    for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
        for _ in range(6):
            L = rng.randint(1, 6)
            hist = [rng.choice(['drive', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
            st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], R=[F(100), F(100)])
            _, y0, _ = wu_run(st0, hist)
            Y0 = share_of(y0)
            d = z1 - z2
            pred = False
            for a in hist:
                if a == 'drive':
                    pred = pred or d != 0
                    d = d * (1 + WU['eta'] * WU['beta'] - WU['gam'])
                else:
                    d = d * (1 - WU['gam'])
            for m in MFAM:
                st1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
                _, y1, _ = wu_run(st1, hist)
                B = B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))
                nw += 1
                vw += (B != ('undef-reading' if 'drive' not in hist else pred))
    return n, viol, nw, vw

def subsegments(n_beats, k):
    size = n_beats // k
    return [list(range(i * size, (i + 1) * size)) for i in range(k)]

def relations_jia(label, st0, hist, brk_sched=None, P=JIA, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), m=m_mean, q=0):
    """Computes D, B_m (share), B_m^q, F(q), Ω_W(q) and Bias(q against the other path) on Family A (or Family A⁺).
    Declared: k equal sub-segments; Pass = number of conducting beats (Qᵢ > 0); the habitual-travel criterion for Familiar Routes and the re-traversal criterion for Ω both take Pass ≥ k; the third item of Ω is that every sub-segment has a conducting beat;
    Brk_W(q) = 1 if and only if brk_q = 1 at some beat within W; Bias: (4a) z_q − z_o > ε_δ at the start of W, (4b) the flow share of q > 1/2 in every sub-segment (operationalization of stable biased continuation over a long window)."""
    o = 1 - q
    segs = subsegments(len(hist), k)
    st, Qs = jia_run(dict(st0), hist, P, brk_sched)
    Y0 = share_of(Qs)
    stI, c, ext = Im_jia(st0, m)
    _, QsI = jia_run(stI, hist, P, brk_sched)
    B = B_value(Y0, share_of(QsI))
    D = st0['z'][0] != st0['z'][1]
    passes = sum(1 for Q in Qs if Q[q] > 0)
    fam = st0['z'][q] > 0 and passes >= k
    # F: comparison task T_k per sub-segment, with the start state taken from the actual run
    Fok = fam
    cur = dict(st0)
    seg_lead = []
    for sg in segs:
        sub = [hist[t] for t in sg]
        sub_brk = [brk_sched[t] for t in sg] if brk_sched else None
        _, Qk = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
        Yk = share_of(Qk)
        cI, _, _ = Im_jia(cur, m)
        _, QkI = jia_run(cI, sub, P, sub_brk)
        YkI = share_of(QkI)
        ld = lead('share', Yk, q + 1)
        bq = Bq_value('share', Yk, YkI, q + 1)
        seg_lead.append((ld, bq))
        Fok = Fok and ld == 'sole lead' and bq is True
        cur, _ = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
~~~
