~~~python
    brkq = any(b[q] for b in brk_sched) if brk_sched else False
    seg_pass = all(any(Qs[t][q] > 0 for t in sg) for sg in segs)
    Om = passes >= k and not brkq and seg_pass
    a4 = st0['z'][q] - st0['z'][o] > eps_d
    b4 = all(sum((Qs[t][q] for t in sg), F(0)) > sum((Qs[t][o] for t in sg), F(0)) for sg in segs)
    Bias = a4 and b4
    return dict(label=label, D=D, B=B, F=Fok, Omega=Om, Bias=Bias, a4=a4, b4=b4, fam=fam, seg=seg_lead, Y0=Y0)

def main():
    print('1. Realization of I_m on Family A and Family E (path-level model (A): path = channel / synapse, Depth = zᵢ)')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], L=[F(1, 5), F(0)])          # state after the H1 drive beat of The Construction Form of Field Concretization (5.1)
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, ext = Im_jia(st, m)
        print(f'  Family A z = (21/10, 1/10), {m.__name__}: c* = {c}, write-back unique (z₁ = z₂ = c*), store L frozen; structural material exchanged with outside B_phys {ext} (positive = added)')
    print('  Physical realization on Family A: the only out-of-boundary action declared in The Construction Form of Field Concretization is a fixed dump of 2 A_u into channel 1 (C5); I_m in general needs an out-of-boundary setting operation that "adds or removes an arbitrary amount", which must be declared separately (aug);')
    print('  once declared, the mass account closes with the out-of-boundary exchange term, and c* ∈ [min, max] ⊂ [0, z_max] stays within the domain of validity.')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], R=[F(19), F(39)])           # H1 of The Construction Form of Field Concretization, Family E: pool 40 → 20 → 19; synapse 2 writes 1/10 in one drive beat, pool 39
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        s2, _, feas2 = Im_wu(st, m, 'frozen')
        print(f'  Family E z = (21/10, 1/10), R = (19, 39), {m.__name__}: c* = {c}; R after R-pool follow-up = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]}), R-pool resource-feasible {feas}; pool-frozen reading resource-feasible {feas2}')
    st = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(5)])
    print('  Family E witness (formally well-defined but not R-pool resource-feasible): z = (2, 0), R = (20, 5):')
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4))):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        print(f'    {m.__name__}: c* = {c}, R′ = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]}), R-pool resource-feasible {feas}')
    print('    R-pool resource-feasibility condition (Theorem W-2; it does not mean that a valid physical setting route already exists, which requires declaring an augmentation operation): c* ≤ min_i [zᵢ + g₁Rᵢ]; here c* ≤ 0 + (1/10)·5 = 1/2, i.e. α ≤ 1/4 in m_α')
    print('2. Finite check of Theorems J-1 / W-1 (share reading)')
    n, viol, nw, vw = thmJ1_check()
    print(f'  Family A (κ_cons ∈ {{0, 1/2, 1}}, γ ∈ {{0, 1/10, 1/2, 1}}, 36 z pairs, 6 random flow/rest histories, 5 m): values {n}, violations {viol}')
    print(f'  Family E (36 z pairs, 6 random drive/rest histories, 5 m): values {nw}, violations {vw}')
    print('3. Two sources of D holding while B_m fails on Family A')
    hist = ['flow'] * 3
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0), F(0)])
    P0 = dict(JIA, kap=F(0))
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P0); _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0)
    print('  law-level (κ_cons = 0, conduction does not read the Trace-Response): Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '→', fmt(share_of(Q1)), '; B_mean =', B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1)))
    brk = [(0, 1)] * 3
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA, brk)
    res = {}
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m)[0], hist, JIA, brk)
        res[m.__name__] = B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1))
    print('  state-level (aug: channel 2 blocked, frozen): Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '; B(min/mean/max) =', res)
    # distinction: B returns once the frozen quantity is changed (blockage lifted); at law level B is false for every value of the frozen quantities
    _, Q0u = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA); _, Q1u = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, JIA)
    print('  distinguishing test (not I_m, but a different counterfactual on the frozen quantities): B_mean after lifting the blockage =', B_value(share_of(Q0u), share_of(Q1u)),
          '; B_mean for every brk ∈ {(0,0), (0,1), (1,0)} when κ_cons = 0 =',
          [B_value(share_of(jia_run(st0, hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]), share_of(jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]))
           for b in ((0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0))])
    print('4. Case table of D, B_m, F, Ω_W, Bias on Family A and Family E (m = mean, 3 sub-segments, ε_δ = 1/100; q = channel / synapse 1)')
    cases = [
        ('A1 baseline (κ_cons = 1, z = (2, 0), flow×6)', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('A2 law-level degeneration (κ_cons = 0)', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, dict(JIA, kap=F(0))),
        ('A3 aug: channel 2 blocked over the whole window', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 1)] * 6, JIA),
        ('A4 aug: channel 1 cut off in the last beat', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 0)] * 5 + [(1, 0)], JIA),
        ('A5 equal depth (z = (1, 1))', dict(z=[F(1), F(1)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('A6 single conduction (flow, rest×5)', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] + ['rest'] * 5, None, JIA),
        ('A7 Trace-Response below threshold (z = (1/200, 0), ε_δ = 1/100)', dict(z=[F(1, 200), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
    ]
    for lab, st0, hist, brk, P in cases:
        r = relations_jia(lab, st0, hist, brk, P)
        print(f'  {lab}: D={r["D"]} B={r["B"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]} (4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}) Familiar Route={r["fam"]}; sub-segments (Lead, B^q) = {r["seg"]}')
    print('  Family E (firing rate, share reading, same parameterized encoding as Family A, Proposition 5 of The Construction Form of Field Concretization):')
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(40)])
    hist = ['drive'] * 6
    _, y0, ok0 = wu_run(dict(st0), hist)
    Y0 = share_of(y0)
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
        _, y1, ok1 = wu_run(s1, hist)
        print(f'    E1 z = (2, 0), drive×6, {m.__name__}: Y = {fmt(Y0)} → {fmt(share_of(y1))}, B = {B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))}, I_m R-pool resource-feasible {feas}, budget within W {ok1}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9  deps15.py  Loads the frozen dependencies and checks their SHA-256 (5.1–5.4)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: loads the frozen dependencies (in the same directory as this program), checking the SHA-256 of each before loading.
Sources (taken verbatim, not one character changed):
  Chapter 11 v1.0 appendix programs: snn_core.py gate_class.py r_layer_witness.py gate_criteria.py lp_exact.py decision.py
  Chapter 12 v1.0 appendix programs: fam_jia_wu.py common12.py
"""
import sys, os, hashlib
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
DEP = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SHA = {
    'snn_core.py': '4e0fd45d2442890ecf3a7687ea285f89ed161e6fb4ab1083ca0fb79821960e49',
    'gate_class.py': 'b07c0327be0383f020de11e03c656e6967df12b972a4e7e97dd0af99885eb630',
    'r_layer_witness.py': '91af0c7f77c0f744ea5f723ba7c7f54cbe4652751d99af617addd0a979e3e1d2',
    'gate_criteria.py': '2429f282f4b83c21113f4f0d412cd8601e11963470aa920f1c9f1b002e61a364',
    'lp_exact.py': '23d6f167ffc1a2e7b49eae57d8d1ac991ace426ea79352740380bba3658c09b4',
    'decision.py': 'c35de9f447bd4bb880b754f537c7c394cac6fa4f108fca6ef98c27df8d528ae1',
    'fam_jia_wu.py': '2131dbfb1d519f0ce9d7f257184aa65ebab9736d7a2b4514b3cb48203c715cd9',

}
~~~

A.10  core15.py  Common module: F-layer evaluation for the linear own-position family, lightweight SNN atoms, law-level process tables and checks of the correspondence conditions, the transducer τ, GS, whole-step commutation, effective-booking truncation and CQ (Section 5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15, *The Mathematical Form of Duty Correspondence in Field Concretization*: common module.
Standard library only; exact rational arithmetic (fractions).
  1. F-layer evaluation of the linear own-position family M(a, b, k, γ, ε) (the F models obtained by compiling Family A and Family E of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization* with J; atoms by D16–D19 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization*;
      also a finite verification of the intervention reading FD against HistCond_F, i.e. Lemma 15-0 of the main text).
  2. SNN (a member of 𝒦_g): beat-by-beat switched runs, lightweight SNN atoms (q, the M1₀ certification of r, the κ of SYN, the effective events acting on T); the full Part and HDyn come from the Chapter 11 adjudicator.
  3. Law-level process tables, the five kinds of maps Φ, and law-level checks of the correspondence conditions MC1–MC5.
  4. The frozen event transducer τ of the main pair, the gating pivot condition GS (sets only available positions), and the whole-step commutation check for linear and bottleneck aggregation.
  5. The F-layer effective-booking truncation Q and the same-branch condition CQ.
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, RW, GK, DC

def fr(q):
    q = F(q)
    return str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}'

def fx(q, n=6):
    return f'{float(q):.{n}f}'

# =====================================================================
# 1. Linear own-position family (two positions; the formation letter puts one out-of-boundary pulse a into position 0; the drive letter writes b + k·z at each position; every beat relaxes by −γ·z)
# =====================================================================
WU = dict(name='Family E', form='pair', drive='drive', rest='rest', wr='hebb', rl='decay',
          a=F(2), b=F(1, 10), k=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), eps=F(1, 1000),
          chain_wr=('syn', 'N', 'syn'), chain_rl=('syn', 'syn'), cut='cut(N→syn)')
JIA = dict(name='Family A', form='seed', drive='flow', rest='rest', wr='write', rl='relax',
           a=F(2), b=F(1, 10), k=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), eps=F(1, 1000),
           chain_wr=('bed', 'flow', 'bed'), chain_rl=('bed', 'bed'), cut='cut(flow→bed)')

def lf_events(M, s, letter, t, sw):
    """Events of one beat (s is the state before the beat). sw: the set of intervention flags for that beat, {'cut', 'freeze', 'freeze_rl'}."""
    ev = []
    if letter == M['form']:
        ev.append(dict(kind='pulse', i=0, t=t, c=M['a'], car='out-of-boundary', k=F(0), cert=1))
    for i in range(2):
        bt, kt = (M['bsched'][t], F(0)) if t in M.get('bsched', {}) else (M['b'], M['k'])
        if letter == M['drive'] and 'freeze' not in sw and (bt != 0 or kt != 0):
            ev.append(dict(kind=M['wr'], i=i, t=t, c=bt + kt * s[i], car='B', k=kt,
                           cert=0 if 'cut' in sw else 1))
        if M['gam'] != 0 and 'freeze' not in sw and 'freeze_rl' not in sw:
            ev.append(dict(kind=M['rl'], i=i, t=t, c=-M['gam'] * s[i], car='B', k=-M['gam'], cert=1))
    return ev

def lf_eval(M, hist, W, T, eps=None, sw=None, erase=None, s0=None, scale=F(1)):
    """F-layer evaluation (D16–D19 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization*; per 4.2, only effective components are booked). scale: depth scale α (a, b and ε are multiplied by α; k and γ are unchanged).
    Returns a dict: atoms, trajectory, in-window events (with Act, Cond, HistCond)."""
    eps = (M['eps'] if eps is None else eps)
    Ms = dict(M, a=M['a'] * scale, b=M['b'] * scale)
    sw = sw or {}
    erase = erase or {}
    s = list(s0) if s0 else [F(0), F(0)]
    form = {0: [], 1: []}
    mark = {0: [], 1: []}
    traj, wev = [list(s)], []
    t0, t1 = W
    for t, letter in enumerate(hist):
        for i in erase.get(t, ()):
            s[i] = F(0)
        evs = lf_events(Ms, s, letter, t, sw.get(t, set()))
        new = list(s)
        for e in evs:
            i, c = e['i'], e['c']
            eff = abs(c) > eps
            e['a'] = 1 if eff else 0
            if e['kind'] == 'pulse':
                if eff:
                    new[i] += c
                    form[i].append(t)
                    mark[i].append(t)
                continue
            avail = (i in T) and s[i] > eps and any(u < t for u in form[i])
            supp_act = e['k'] != 0
            supp_cond = supp_act or (abs(c) <= eps)
            e['hist'] = 1 if (avail and supp_act and e['cert'] == 1) else 0
            e['cond'] = 1 if (avail and supp_cond) else 0
            e['avail'] = avail
            if eff:
                if s[i] <= eps < s[i] + c:
                    form[i].append(t)
                new[i] += c
            if t0 <= t <= t1:
                wev.append(e)
        assert all(v >= 0 for v in new), 'out of the domain of definition (D20)'
        s = new
        traj.append(list(s))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[i] for i in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[i] > eps and form[i] for i in T) else 0
    Part = 1 if any(e['cond'] for e in wev if e['car'] == 'B') else 0
    HD = [e for e in wev if e['i'] in T and e['a'] and e['hist']]
    HDyn = 1 if HD else 0
    Q = Src & Ret & Part & HDyn
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Part=Part, HDyn=HDyn, Q=Q, inner=HDyn, traj=traj, wev=wev, form=form, mark=mark,
                witness=[(e['kind'], e['i'], e['t']) for e in HD])

def lf_fd(M, hist, W, T, e, cap=F(10), eps=None):
    """Intervention reading: at beats u ≤ t(e), set each available T position to several rational points in [0, cap] and rerun up to t(e); if the event vanishes or its component changes, FD = 1 (finite verification)."""
    eps = M['eps'] if eps is None else eps
    base = lf_eval(M, hist, (0, len(hist) - 1), {0, 1}, eps)
    for u in range(0, e['t'] + 1):
        su = base['traj'][u]
        av = [i for i in T if su[i] > eps and any(v < u for v in base['form'][i])]
        for i in av:
            for val in (F(0), su[i] / 2, su[i] * 2, cap):
                if val == su[i]:
                    continue
                s0 = list(su)
                s0[i] = val
                r = lf_eval(M, hist[u:], (0, len(hist) - 1 - u), {0, 1}, eps, s0=s0)
                cand = [x for x in r['wev'] if x['kind'] == e['kind'] and x['i'] == e['i'] and x['t'] + u == e['t']]
                if not cand or cand[0]['c'] != e['c']:
                    return 1
    return 0

# =====================================================================
# 2. SNN (𝒦_g) helpers
# =====================================================================
NONE = GK.NONE
TPOS = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]

def snn_run(G, hist, sw=None, o0=None):
    """Beat-by-beat switched run. sw: beat → (plastic, bap); default (True, True). Returns (table of pre-beat states, event table, gate table)."""
    sw = sw or {}
    o = o0 or G.init()
    pre, evs, gates = {}, {}, {}
    for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
        pl, bp = sw.get(t, (True, True))
        pre[t] = o
        evs[t] = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t, pl, bp)
        o, rec = G.step(o, sig, plastic=pl, bap=bp)
        gates[t] = rec['g']
    pre[len(hist)] = o
    return pre, evs, gates

def zk(G, o, k):
    return o[0][k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]

def snn_form_avail(G, pre, evs, T, n, eps):
    form = {k: [] for k in T}
    for t in range(n):
        for e in evs[t].values():
            k = e['tgt']
            if k in T and abs(e['c']) > eps and zk(G, pre[t], k) <= eps < zk(G, pre[t], k) + e['c']:
                form[k].append(t)
    avail = {k: {t for t in range(n + 1) if zk(G, pre[t], k) > eps and any(u < t for u in form[k])} for k in T}
    return form, avail

def snn_light(G, hist, t0, T=TPOS, sw=None, eps=F(1, 100)):
    """Lightweight SNN atoms: q; the effective events in W acting on T; the κ borne by SYN (SYN reads an available T position; setting that position back to baseline in the same beat changes the component, so the read edge is reachable)."""
    pre, evs, gates = snn_run(G, hist, sw)
    n = len(hist)
    form, avail = snn_form_avail(G, pre, evs, T, n, eps)
    q = 1 if all(zk(G, pre[n], k) > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    teff = [e for t in range(t0, n) for e in evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] in T and abs(e['c']) > eps]
    syn_k = [e for t in range(t0, n) for e in evs[t].values()
             if e['id'][0] == 'SYN' and ('w',) + e['id'][1] in T and t in avail[('w',) + e['id'][1]]]
    return dict(q=q, teff=teff, kappa_syn=1 if syn_k else 0, n_syn=len(syn_k), pre=pre, evs=evs, gates=gates,
                form=form, avail=avail)

def withdrawals(G, sig):
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    pats = [nm for nm, p in G.pat.items() if all(p[i] <= s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))]
    nz = [j for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != 0]
    out = []
    for nm in pats:
        for m in range(1 << len(nz)):
            I2 = list(I)
            for b, j in enumerate(nz):
                if m >> b & 1:
                    I2[j] = 0
            cand = (nm, tuple(I2))
            if cand != (name, tuple(I)):
                out.append(cand)
    return out

def snn_r_M1(G, hist, T=TPOS, eps=F(1, 100), sw=None):
    """Same-beat admissible-removal certification M1₀ of r (the M1₀ branch of criterion PS of Chapter 11): an effective write acting on T is counted as certified if some same-beat removal satisfies the no-opening condition and changes its occurrence or component."""
    pre, evs, gates = snn_run(G, hist, sw)
    sw = sw or {}
    cert, tot = 0, 0
    for t in range(len(hist)):
        pl, bp = sw.get(t, (True, True))
        for e in evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in T or abs(e['c']) <= eps or e['id'][0] not in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX'):
                continue
            tot += 1
            for sig2 in withdrawals(G, hist[t]):
                _, rec = G.step(pre[t], sig2, plastic=pl, bap=bp)
                if any(rec['g'][j] > gates[t][j] for j in range(G.n_out)):
                    continue
                e2 = RW.Ev(G, pre[t], sig2, t, pl, bp).get(e['id'])
                if e2 is None or e2['c'] != e['c']:
                    cert += 1
                    break
    return dict(r=1 if cert else 0, cert=cert, tot=tot)

def snn_full(name, G, hist, t0, T=TPOS):
    """Part and HDyn from the Chapter 11 adjudicator (exact adjudication by 𝒟₁, declared B_FD); returns (Part, HDyn, rows of in-window events acting on T)."""
    I = GK.Instance(name, G, hist, t0, T=T)
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    res = DC.judge(I, D, True)
    rows = [(r['e']['id'][0] + ''.join(map(str, r['e']['id'][1])) + '@' + str(r['e']['t']), r['e']['c'], r['a'], r['v'], r['tier'])
            for r in res['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in T]
    return res['Part'], res['HDyn'], rows, I

# =====================================================================
# 3. Law-level process tables, Φ and the MC checks
# =====================================================================
# Fields: role (+write / −depression / −relaxation / read-out / formation / auxiliary), car, tgt (T / aux), reads_T (the law reads T positions), reads_aux (unmapped auxiliary quantities the law reads),
#       mode (set of law-level modes of determination: occ occurrence, str strength), law (law type), chain (node classes of the route), off (interventions that make it not occur), cut (interventions that set its route certification to 0)
SNN_TABLE = {
    'PLUS': dict(role='+write', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'x', 'w_nonT'}, mode={'occ'},
                 law='gated step', chain=('synapse', 'output unit', 'synapse'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
    'MINUS': dict(role='−depression', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'y', 'w_nonT'}, mode={'str'},
                  law='gated step (y′ = d·y + g)', chain=('synapse', 'output unit', 'synapse'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
    'SYN': dict(role='read-out', car='B', tgt='aux', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='linear (k = 1)',
                chain=('synapse', 'output unit'), off=set(), cut=set()),
    'FORM': dict(role='formation', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=False, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'x'}, mode=set(), law='gated step (teaching pairing)',
                 chain=('out-of-boundary stimulus', 'entry unit', 'output unit', 'synapse'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
}
WU_TABLE = {
    'hebb': dict(role='+write', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='linear (b + k·z)',
                 chain=('synapse', 'postsynaptic neuron', 'synapse'), off={'freeze'}, cut={'cut'}),
    'decay': dict(role='−relaxation', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='linear (−γ·z)',
                  chain=('synapse', 'synapse'), off={'freeze'}, cut=set()),
    'pair': dict(role='formation', car='out-of-boundary', tgt='T', reads_T=False, reads_aux=set(), mode=set(), law='pulse',
                 chain=('out-of-boundary electrode', 'synapse'), off=set(), cut=set()),
}
JIA_TABLE = {
    'write': dict(WU_TABLE['hebb'], chain=('wall', 'channel carrier fluid', 'wall')),
    'relax': dict(WU_TABLE['decay'], chain=('wall', 'wall')),
    'seed': dict(WU_TABLE['pair'], chain=('out-of-boundary dump point', 'wall')),
}
NODE_MAP_MAIN = {'synapse': 'synapse', 'output unit': 'postsynaptic neuron', 'out-of-boundary stimulus': 'out-of-boundary electrode', 'entry unit': None}
NODE_MAP_ENC = {'wall': 'synapse', 'channel carrier fluid': 'postsynaptic neuron', 'out-of-boundary dump point': 'out-of-boundary electrode'}

def role_class(r):
    return '+' if r.startswith('+') else ('−' if r.startswith('−') else r)

def mc_check(tab1, tab2, phiE, node_map, phiI, eps1, eps2, alpha, lam1=None, premise2=None):
    """Law-level checks of MC1–MC5 (sub-items). Returns [(clause, holds or not, explanation)]."""
    out = []
    T1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['tgt'] == 'T' and d['role'] != 'formation']
    T2 = [k for k, d in tab2.items() if d['tgt'] == 'T' and d['role'] != 'formation']
    bad = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2 or role_class(tab1[k]['role']) != role_class(tab2[phiE[k]]['role'])
           or tab1[k]['car'] != tab2[phiE[k]]['car']]
    surj = set(phiE.get(k) for k in T1) >= set(T2)
    out.append(('MC1-W (in-window processes acting on T: duty class, carrier and target correspond; surjective onto the other side’s processes acting on T)', not bad and surj,
                f'mismatches {bad}; surjective {surj}'))
    f1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['role'] == 'formation']
    fb = [k for k in f1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2 or tab2[phiE[k]]['car'] != tab1[k]['car']]
    out.append(('MC1-F (carrier attribution of formation processes corresponds)', not fb,
                '; '.join(f'{k}: car {tab1[k]["car"]} ↦ {phiE.get(k)}: car {tab2[phiE[k]]["car"] if phiE.get(k) in tab2 else "—"}' for k in f1)))
    p1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['car'] == 'B' and d['reads_T'] and d['tgt'] != 'T']
    pb = [k for k in p1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2]
    out.append(('MC1-P (processes inside B that read T and act outside T have images)', not pb, f'no image {pb}'))
    ra = [k for k in T1 if tab1[k]['reads_aux'] and phiE.get(k) in tab2 and not tab2[phiE[k]]['reads_aux']]
    out.append(('MC2-r (read sets correspond: the auxiliary quantities and non-T positions the law reads have images)', not ra,
                '; '.join(f'{k} reads unmapped {sorted(tab1[k]["reads_aux"])}' for k in ra)))
    mm = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) in tab2 and tab1[k]['mode'] != tab2[phiE[k]]['mode']]
    out.append(('MC2-m (same law-level mode of determination)', not mm,
                '; '.join(f'{k} {sorted(tab1[k]["mode"])} ↦ {phiE[k]} {sorted(tab2[phiE[k]]["mode"])}' for k in mm)))
    lt = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) in tab2 and tab1[k]['law'].split(' (')[0] != tab2[phiE[k]]['law'].split(' (')[0]]
    out.append(('necessary part of MC2⁺ (same law type)', not lt,
                '; '.join(f'{k} {tab1[k]["law"]} ↦ {tab2[phiE[k]]["law"]}' for k in lt)))
    cb = []
    for k in T1:
        k2 = phiE.get(k)
        if k2 not in tab2:
            continue
        c1, c2 = tab1[k]['chain'], tab2[k2]['chain']
        img = tuple(node_map.get(n, n) for n in c1)
        if img != c2:
            cb.append(f'{k}: {"→".join(c1)} ↦ {"→".join(map(str, img))} ≠ {"→".join(c2)}')
    out.append(('MC3 (route-chain nodes correspond, all inside B, forward in time)', not cb, '; '.join(cb) or 'node-by-node correspondence'))
    for I1, I2 in phiI.items():
        dis1 = {phiE.get(k) for k in T1 if I1 in tab1[k]['off']}
        cut1 = {phiE.get(k) for k in T1 if I1 in tab1[k]['cut']}
        dis2 = {k for k in T2 if I2 in tab2[k]['off']}
        cut2 = {k for k in T2 if I2 in tab2[k]['cut']}
        ok = dis1 == dis2 and cut1 == cut2
        out.append((f'MC4 ({I1} ↦ {I2})', ok,
                    f'one side: prevents from occurring {sorted(x for x in dis1 if x)}, sets route certification to 0 {sorted(x for x in cut1 if x)}; '
                    f'other side: prevents from occurring {sorted(dis2)}, sets route certification to 0 {sorted(cut2)}'))
    out.append(('MC5-ε (ε₂ = α·ε₁)', eps2 == alpha * eps1, f'ε₁ = {fr(eps1)}, ε₂ = {fr(eps2)}, α = {fr(alpha)}'))
    if lam1 is not None:
        out.append(('MC5-τ (timescale antecedent: the other side’s “membrane time constant ≪ Δt” holds on this side’s beats, i.e. λ = 0 or a block map realizes it)', lam1 == 0,
                    f'λ = {fr(lam1)}; for block maps not realizing it see Theorem 15-10 (MD-2)'))
    return out

# =====================================================================
# 4. Frozen transducer τ, GS, whole-step commutation of aggregation
# =====================================================================
FORM_LETTER = ('A', (F(1), 0))

def tau(hist):
    """Frozen event transducer τ of the main pair (D15-5): one bit of state f (whether a formation image has already been output), initially 0. Beat by beat:
      the letter is exactly (A, teaching (1, 0)): if f = 0, output pair and set f = 1; if f = 1, output rest;
      among the other letters, those that open entries 0 and 1 together (A, AB, any teaching current) output drive;
      the rest (B, rest, letters with teaching current only) output rest.
    The output depends only on the letter prefix: it is a deterministic sequence function (a Mealy machine), not a letter-wise static function."""
    f, out = 0, []
    for name, I in hist:
        if (name, tuple(F(x) for x in I)) == (FORM_LETTER[0], tuple(F(x) for x in FORM_LETTER[1])):
            out.append('rest' if f else 'pair')
            f = 1
        elif name in ('A', 'AB'):
            out.append('drive')
        else:
            out.append('rest')
    return out

def gs(G, o, sig, T, avail, j=0):
    """Gating pivot condition GS(t, j) (D15-10): after the T positions read by j in this beat and available at t are set back to baseline,
    gate argument < θ_j ≤ actual gate argument. Returns (GS, actual vt, vt after setting back)."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
    vtb = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum((G.b[i][j] if (('w', i, j) in T and ('w', i, j) in avail) else w[i][j]) * s[i]
                                for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
    return vtb < G.theta[j] <= vt, vt, vtb

def split_step(M, xs, letter, a_split):
    """One beat of the four-position source model (each synapse of Family E split into two parallel sub-synapses, same law as Family E, each reading its own position), at the mechanism layer (no effectiveness-threshold truncation).
    Returns (post-beat state, list of components per position)."""
    cs = [[] for _ in xs]
    if letter == M['form']:
        cs[0].append(a_split[0]); cs[1].append(a_split[1])
    for i, z in enumerate(xs):
        if letter == M['drive']:
            cs[i].append(M['b'] + M['k'] * z)
        cs[i].append(-M['gam'] * z)
    return [z + sum(c) for z, c in zip(xs, cs)], cs

def wu_mech_step(M, zs, letter, a):
    cs = [[] for _ in zs]
    if letter == M['form']:
        cs[0].append(a)
    for i, z in enumerate(zs):
        if letter == M['drive']:
            cs[i].append(M['b'] + M['k'] * z)
        cs[i].append(-M['gam'] * z)
    return [z + sum(c) for z, c in zip(zs, cs)], cs

# =====================================================================
# 5. F-layer effective-booking truncation and its commutation with aggregation
# =====================================================================
def Qe(c, eps):
    """F-layer effective booking Q(c) = c·[|c| > ε] (*The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization*, 4.2)."""
    return c if abs(c) > eps else F(0)

def cq_condition(cs, eps):
    """Same-branch condition CQ of Lemma 15-1a (F branch): a family of preimage components is either all effective with the same sign, or all ineffective (|c| ≤ ε)."""
    if all(abs(c) <= eps for c in cs):
        return True
    return all(abs(c) > eps for c in cs) and (all(c > 0 for c in cs) or all(c < 0 for c in cs))
~~~

A.11  run15\_main.py  Run entry: law-level checks on the main pair and the boundary pair, atoms on both sides, preservation register (10.13, 10.16)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: law-level checks of MC1–MC5, the atoms on both sides, and the preservation register on the main pair (Instance P (s1 parameters) ↔ Family E) and the boundary pair (Family A ↔ Family E).
Run: python3 -B run15_main.py > run15_main.txt
"""
import sys, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, GK, FJW, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
G = GC.snn_gate(GC.load_snn_core()[0])
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * 6
REST = [C.NONE] * 8
HP = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()          # Instance P (Chapter 11, 7.5)

def phi_sigma(sig, form_once=False, t=None):
    """Letter map φ_Σ (declared): A+teach (1,0) ↦ pair (formation); A, AB ↦ drive (presynaptic firing, restricted to the mapped entries 0 and 1); B, rest ↦ rest.
    Teaching current has no image outside the formation letter (Family E has no injection into the postsynaptic neuron). form_once: map only the first formation letter to pair; the other formation letters ↦ rest."""
    name, I = sig
    if name == 'A' and tuple(I) == (F(1), 0):
        return 'pair' if (not form_once or t == 0) else 'rest'
    if name in ('A', 'AB'):
        return 'drive'
    return 'rest'

print('\n1. Declaration of the main pair')
print('  𝔐₁ = the R-layer declared instance of Definitions 4–7 of Chapter 11, Instance P (s1 frozen declared history: formation (A+teach(1,0), rest, rest)×6, rest 8, W = beats 26–43 as (A,rest,rest,B,rest,rest)×3; '
      'T₁ = {w00, w10}, ε₁ = 1/100).')
H2 = [phi_sigma(s) for s in HP]
H2b = C.tau(HP)                      # frozen transducer τ (D15-5); asserted to agree with the beat-by-beat formulation
assert H2b == [phi_sigma(s, True, t) for t, s in enumerate(HP)]
print('  𝔐₂ = the encoding J(χ_E) of Family E of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization* (a = 2, b = k = 1/10, γ = 1/10, ε₂ = 1/1000), history h₂ = φ_Σ(h₁), T₂ = {z1}, W at the same beats.')
print('  h₂ =', ' '.join(H2))
print('  Φ_main: φ_Ξ is the path aggregation of the bifurcation reading (path 0 = {w00, w10} ↦ z1, path 1 = {w01, w11} ↦ z2, Depth = arithmetic mean of z within a path, depth scale α); '
      'φ_E: PLUS ↦ hebb, MINUS ↦ decay, formation write ↦ pair, SYN and auxiliary processes have no image; φ_t is the identity beat map; '
      'φ_I: bap off ↦ cut(N→syn), plastic off ↦ freeze, erasure (w ← b) ↦ z ← 0, 𝒟₁ setting σ ↦ path-mean setting.')
print('\n2. Law-level checks of MC1–MC5 (main pair)')
PHI_E = {'PLUS': 'hebb', 'MINUS': 'decay', 'FORM': 'pair', 'SYN': None}
PHI_I = {'bap_off': 'cut', 'plastic_off': 'freeze'}
for alpha in (F(1), F(1, 10)):
    print(f'  [α = {C.fr(alpha)}]')
    for name, ok, why in C.mc_check(C.SNN_TABLE, C.WU_TABLE, PHI_E, C.NODE_MAP_MAIN, PHI_I, F(1, 100), F(1, 1000), alpha,
                                    lam1=G.lam[0]):
        print(f'    {"holds" if ok else "fails"} | {name} | {why}')
print('\n3. Atoms on both sides (model mode; the SNN evaluated with R-layer witnesses, Family E by D16–D19 of the F layer)')
Lt = C.snn_light(G, HP, 26)
rM = C.snn_r_M1(G, HP)
Part1, HDyn1, rows1, I1 = C.snn_full('P', G, HP, 26)
print(f'  𝔐₁: q = {Lt["q"]}; r = {rM["r"]} ({rM["tot"]} effective writes acting on T, {rM["cert"]} certified by M1₀); '
      f'Part = {Part1} ({Lt["n_syn"]} events in which SYN bears κ); HDyn = {HDyn1}; Q = {Lt["q"] & rM["r"] & Part1 & HDyn1}')
print('    in-window events acting on T: ' + '; '.join(f'{a} c={C.fx(c)} a={aa} η={v}({tier})' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows1))
for lab, h2 in (('h₂ = φ_Σ⁰(h₁) (static letter function, six pairs)', H2), ('h₂′ = τ(h₁) (frozen transducer, first pair only)', H2b)):
    R2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, h2, (26, 43), {0})
    st = dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(40), F(40)])
    st, ys, ok = FJW.wu_run(st, h2)
    print(f'  𝔐₂ {lab}: Src = {R2["Src"]}, Ret = {R2["Ret"]}, Part = {R2["Part"]}, HDyn = {R2["HDyn"]}, Q = {R2["Q"]}, in-boundary self-feedback = {R2["inner"]}; '
          f'z1 at beat 26 = {C.fx(R2["traj"][26][0])}; HDyn witnesses {R2["witness"][:4]}…; '
          f'physical check with Chapter 12 wu_run (initial receptor pool 40): {"pass" if ok else "fail"}, final pools {[C.fx(r, 2) for r in st["R"]]}')
    fdok = all(C.lf_fd(C.WU, h2, (26, 43), {0}, e) == e['hist'] for e in R2['wev'] if e['i'] == 0 and e['avail'])
    print(f'    finite verification of Lemma 15-0 (Family E events in the window acting on z1 with z1 available: intervention reading FD = HistCond_F): {fdok}')
print('\n4. Preservation register for the main pair (equal values are not certification by MC; for the basis of certification see the theorems in the main text)')
R2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, H2, (26, 43), {0})
av2 = all(e['avail'] for e in R2['wev'] if e['kind'] == 'hebb' and e['i'] == 0)
reg = [
    ('q (Ret)', Lt['q'], R2['Ret'], 'state correspondence + formation correspondence (near-definitional, not counted as an increment); the main pair has no run correspondence, so the equal values are not certified by MC'),
    ('r (Src)', rM['r'], R2['Src'], 'MC1-F fails; cross-route MC3ˢ gives Pres⁺ for 1→2 (target-side determined, Theorem 15-7)'),
    ('Part', Part1, R2['Part'], 'preservation of Part not issued; Theorem 15-5 gives only “source HDyn = 1 ⇒ target Part = 1” (source HDyn = 1 holds here); for the frozen control see run15_regress, Part 3'),
    ('Act (per event)', 1, 1, 'amplitude correspondence of MC2⁺ fails; the law-level lower bound gives |c| ≥ b = 1/10 > ε₂ for the drive-beat writes of Family E'),
    ('HistCond (per event)', 1, 1, f'1→2: z1 available gives η₂ = 1 (Lemma 15-0, AV₂ = {av2}); 2→1: domain partition by Theorem 15-15 on the pure class 𝒞₁ᵖ(n) with fixed n, ambiguous on the fibre containing the given common image (Theorem 15-8)'),
    ('HDyn', HDyn1, R2['HDyn'], 'Instance P belongs to no 𝒞₁ᵖ(n) (the window has B beats); its τ-fibre is not pure in classes containing AB (run15_regress, Part 5); equal values, transfer not issued'),
    ('Q', Lt['q'] & rM['r'] & Part1 & HDyn1, R2['Q'], 'equal values, transfer not issued'),
    ('in-boundary self-feedback', 1, R2['inner'], 'B-attribution of the nodes in MC3 (PLUS↔hebb) corresponds; follows HDyn'),
]
for a, v1, v2, why in reg:
    print(f'  {a} | 𝔐₁ = {v1} | 𝔐₂ = {v2} | {why}')
print('\n5. Boundary pair (Family A ↔ Family E, Φ_enc: the renaming seed↔pair, flow↔drive, write↔hebb, relax↔decay of Proposition 5 of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization*, α = 1)')
PHI_E2 = {'write': 'hebb', 'relax': 'decay', 'seed': 'pair'}
PHI_I2 = {'cut_flow_bed': 'cut'}
tabJ = {k: dict(v, cut={'cut_flow_bed'} if k == 'write' else set()) for k, v in C.JIA_TABLE.items()}
for name, ok, why in C.mc_check(tabJ, C.WU_TABLE, PHI_E2, C.NODE_MAP_ENC, PHI_I2, F(1, 1000), F(1, 1000), F(1)):
    print(f'  {"holds" if ok else "fails"} | {name} | {why}')
ren = {'seed': 'pair', 'flow': 'drive', 'rest': 'rest'}
n_eq, n_tot, n_pos = 0, 0, 0
for L in range(1, 5):
    for h in itertools.product(('seed', 'flow', 'rest'), repeat=L):
        for t0 in range(L):
            for t1 in range(t0, L):
                for T in ({0}, {1}, {0, 1}):
                    for sw in ({}, {t: {'cut'} for t in range(L)}):
                        a = C.lf_eval(C.JIA, list(h), (t0, t1), T, sw=sw)
                        b = C.lf_eval(C.WU, [ren[x] for x in h], (t0, t1), T, sw=sw)
                        keys = ('Src', 'Ret', 'Part', 'HDyn', 'Q', 'inner')
                        n_tot += 1
                        n_eq += all(a[k] == b[k] for k in keys) and a['traj'] == b['traj']
                        n_pos += a['Q']
print(f'  all histories of length 1–4 × all windows × T ∈ {{z1}},{{z2}},{{z1,z2}} × (no intervention / route cut throughout): {n_tot} groups, atoms and trajectories identical item by item in {n_eq}, of which {n_pos} have Q = 1.')
st40, _, ok40 = FJW.wu_run(dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(40), F(40)]), ['pair', 'drive'])
st10, _, ok10 = FJW.wu_run(dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(10), F(10)]), ['pair', 'drive'])
e40 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, ['pair', 'drive'], (1, 1), {0})
print(f'  The physical layer is not preserved: for the Family E history (pair, drive), initial receptor pools 40 and 10 have the same encoding and the same Q = {e40["Q"]}, physical checks {ok40} and {ok10} (final pool {C.fx(st10["R"][0], 2)}) — '
      'encoding isomorphism does not read back physical validity (four-layer separation (C) of *The Construction Form of Field Concretization*; the preservation theorems of this paper do not cross the physical layer).')
for m, nm in ((FJW.m_min, 'min'), (FJW.m_mean, 'mean'), (FJW.m_max, 'max')):
    stj = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], L=[F(1, 5), F(0)])
    stw = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], R=[F(19), F(39)])
    _, c, ext = FJW.Im_jia(stj, m)
    _, c2, feas = FJW.Im_wu(stw, m)
    print(f'  I_{nm} (z = (21/10, 1/10)): c* = {C.fr(c)}; Family A must exchange structural material {C.fr(ext)} with the outside of B_phys (not realizable within the current declaration); Family E R-pool resource-feasible {feas} (setting route undeclared)')
~~~

A.12  run15\_witness.py  Run entry: fibre witnesses, GS, the super-beat encoding obstruction, the rate staircase, MC5-ε, intervention commutation, insufficiency of actual-run correspondence, unidirectionality, composition and scale conjugacy (Sections 10 and 11)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: exact witnesses of the failure boundaries and finite verification of the theorems.
Run: python3 -B run15_witness.py > run15_witness.txt
Part 1, fibre witness (Theorem 15-8); Part 2, sufficiency of the gating pivot condition GS (Theorem 15-8 (3)); Part 3, the super-beat encoding obstruction (Theorem 15-10 (1), MD-1);
Part 4, the staircase character of the rate limit (Theorem 15-10 (2), MD-2); Part 5, MC5-ε is necessary; Part 6, MC4 intervention commutation; Part 7, actual-run correspondence is insufficient (MC2-r is necessary) and MC4-𝒟 is necessary;
Part 8, unidirectionality; Part 9, composition, inversion and scale conjugacy of 𝒦_g (positive witnesses of Theorems 15-1–15-3).
"""
import sys, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, RW, GK, DC, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
S0 = GC.load_snn_core()[0]
G = GC.snn_gate(S0)
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * 6
REST = [C.NONE] * 8
T = C.TPOS

def gs(G, o, sig, T, j=0, avail=None):
    """Calls core15.gs (sets only the available T positions back to baseline). By default avail treats all of T as available (the caller checks availability separately)."""
    return C.gs(G, o, sig, T, set(T) if avail is None else avail, j)

print('\n1. Fibre witness (Theorem 15-8): the same image under the same frozen transducer τ and the same trajectory on T beat by beat, yet different HDyn on the SNN side')
WS = {'S_A': [('A', (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE], 'S_AB': [('AB', (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE],
      'S_I': [('A', (F(1, 2), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE]}
res = {}
for nm, wl in WS.items():
    h = FORM + REST + wl
    P, H, rows, I = C.snn_full(nm, G, h, 26)
    ok, vt, vtb = gs(G, I.pre[26], h[26], T, avail={k for k in T if I.is_avail(k, 26)})
    res[nm] = (P, H, I)
    print(f'  {nm}: letter at beat 26 {h[26]}; Part = {P}, HDyn = {H}; GS = {ok} (actual gate argument {C.fx(vt)}, with T set back to baseline {C.fx(vtb)}, θ = 1)')
    print('    in-window events acting on T: ' + '; '.join(f'{a} c={C.fx(c)} a={aa} η={v}({tier})' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows))
same = all(res['S_A'][2].pre[t][0][i][0] == res[n][2].pre[t][0][i][0] and res['S_A'][2].pre[t][2][:2] == res[n][2].pre[t][2][:2]
           and res['S_A'][2].pre[t][3][0] == res[n][2].pre[t][3][0]
           for n in ('S_AB', 'S_I') for t in range(30) for i in (0, 1))
print(f'  the three agree beat by beat (beats 0–29) on the T positions (w00, w10), on the eligibility traces x0, x1 of the mapped entries and on the postsynaptic trace y0 of output 0: {same}')

imgs = {nm: tuple(C.tau(FORM + REST + wl)) for nm, wl in WS.items()}
print(f'  the three have the same image under the frozen transducer τ: {len(set(imgs.values())) == 1}; pair beats of the image {[t for t, x in enumerate(imgs["S_A"]) if x == "pair"]}, last three beats {imgs["S_A"][-3:]}')
r2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, list(imgs['S_A']), (26, 28), {0})
print(f'  image instance (Family E): Src = {r2["Src"]}, Ret = {r2["Ret"]}, Part = {r2["Part"]}, HDyn = {r2["HDyn"]}, Q = {r2["Q"]}. '
      'Hence HDyn₁ takes both 1 and 0 on the τ-fibre of this common image: every recovery rule that takes the τ-image as input must err on this fibre, and every condition set that certifies 2→1 preservation must exclude it (Theorem 15-8, issued for this fibre only); the difference lies in unmapped gating reads (the non-T positions w20, w30 and the teaching current I0).')
G3 = GC.Gate(G.n_in, G.n_out, G.pat, G.lam, [F(3), F(1)], G.vreset, G.dx, G.dy, G.Ap, G.Am, G.rho, G.b, G.lo, G.hi)
h3 = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
L3 = C.snn_light(G3, h3, 26)
r3 = C.snn_r_M1(G3, h3)
r2b = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h3), (26, 43), {0})
print(f'  fibre witness for an unmapped parameter: change only the threshold of output 0 to θ₀ = 3 (τ-image the same as for Instance P): SNN q = {L3["q"]}, r = {r3["r"]} ({r3["tot"]} effective writes acting on T), '
      f'{len(L3["teff"])} effective in-window events acting on T ⇒ HDyn = 0; image instance Src = {r2b["Src"]}, Ret = {r2b["Ret"]}, HDyn = {r2b["HDyn"]} — on the same image none of q, r, HDyn is determined by the image.')
print('\n2. Sufficiency of GS (Theorem 15-8 (3); sets only available positions): GS holds ⇒ setting T back to baseline in the same beat closes the gate, PLUS does not occur, and the read edge is reachable ⇒ η(PLUS) = 1 (finite verification)')
cnt = dict(gs=0, ok=0, nogs=0)
for W0 in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)):
    for Ap in (F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
        for teach in (F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)):
            for n in (1, 3, 6):
                for X in ('A', 'AB'):
                    S0.W0, S0.A_PLUS = W0, Ap
                    Gx = GC.snn_gate(S0)
                    h = [('A', (teach, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * n + [C.NONE] * 8 + [(X, (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE]
                    t = 3 * n + 8
                    L = C.snn_light(Gx, h, t)
                    o = L['pre'][t]
                    ok, vt, vtb = gs(Gx, o, h[t], T, avail={k for k in T if t in L['avail'][k]})
                    if not ok:
                        cnt['nogs'] += 1
                        continue
                    cnt['gs'] += 1
                    av = all(t in L['avail'][k] for k in T)
                    o2 = GK.setw(o, {k: Gx.b[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
                    ev2 = RW.Ev(Gx, o2, h[t], t)
                    plus_gone = ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) in L['evs'][t] and ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) not in ev2
                    reads = ('w', 0, 0) in L['evs'][t][('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]['reads']
                    cnt['ok'] += bool(av and plus_gone and reads)
S0.W0, S0.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
print(f'  parameter grid W0 × A₊ × teaching × number of formations × first window letter (A / AB), 216 cases: GS holds in {cnt["gs"]}, of which {cnt["ok"]} have “T positions available, PLUS vanishes when set back to baseline, PLUS reads the T position”; GS fails in {cnt["nogs"]} (η = 0 is not inferred from this: GS is only a sufficient condition).')
print('\n3. Super-beat encoding obstruction (Theorem 15-10 (1)): the block sum Σ PLUS as a function of the setting value σ is piecewise constant, and the jump J at the first gating switch within the block does not lie in εℤ')
h = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
pre, evs, gates = C.snn_run(G, h)
u = 26

def block(sig_val, L, which='plus'):
    o = GK.setw(pre[u], {('w', 0, 0): sig_val, ('w', 1, 0): sig_val})
    tot = F(0)
    for t in range(u, u + L):
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, h[t], t)
        if which == 'plus':
            tot += e[('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]['c'] if ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) in e else 0
        o, _ = G.step(o, h[t])
    return tot if which == 'plus' else o[0][0][0] - sig_val

N = 280
grid = [G.b[0][0] + (G.hi[0][0] - G.b[0][0]) * F(j, N) for j in range(N + 1)]
for L in (1, 6, 12, 18):
    vals = [block(sv, L) for sv in grid]
    jumps = [j for j in range(N) if vals[j] != vals[j + 1]]
    j0 = jumps[0]
    lo, hi_ = grid[j0], grid[j0 + 1]
    for _ in range(40):
        mid = (lo + hi_) / 2
        if block(mid, L) == vals[j0]:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi_ = mid
    J = block(hi_, L) - block(lo, L)
    Jw = block(hi_, L, 'w') - block(lo, L, 'w')
    print(f'  block length L = {L:2d}: on the {N + 1} grid points Σ PLUS00 takes {len(set(vals))} distinct values; first switch point σ* ∈ ({C.fx(lo, 9)}, {C.fx(hi_, 9)}); '
          f'block-sum jump J = {C.fx(J)}, J/ε = {C.fx(J * 100, 4)} (∉ ℤ: {(J * 100).denominator != 1}); jump of the w00 block increment {C.fx(Jw)}')
print('\n4. Staircase character of the rate limit (Theorem 15-10 (2)): a gating unit under constant drive u, reset to 0, has firing period n(u) = min{n : u·Σ_{k<n} λ^k ≥ θ} and rate 1/n(u)')

def n_of(u_, lam, th=F(1), nmax=400):
    s, p = F(0), F(1)
    for n in range(1, nmax + 1):
        s += p
        p *= lam
        if u_ * s >= th:
            return n
    return None

def sim_rate(u_, lam, L=240, th=F(1)):
    v, cnt_ = F(0), 0
    for _ in range(L):
        vt = lam * v + u_
        if vt >= th:
            cnt_ += 1
            v = F(0)
        else:
            v = vt
    return cnt_

bad = 0
for lam in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)):
    us = [F(j, 200) for j in range(1, 401)]
    rs = []
    for u_ in us:
        n = n_of(u_, lam)
        r = F(0) if n is None else F(1, n)
        c = sim_rate(u_, lam)
        if n is not None and c != 240 // n:
            bad += 1
        rs.append(r)
    distinct = sorted(set(rs))
    jmax = max(rs[i + 1] - rs[i] for i in range(len(rs) - 1))
    mono = all(rs[i] <= rs[i + 1] for i in range(len(rs) - 1))
    print(f'  λ = {C.fr(lam)}: on the 400 grid points of u ∈ (0, 2] the rate takes {len(distinct)} values, monotone nondecreasing {mono}, maximum jump {C.fr(jmax)}; '
          f'the uniform error of approximation by any affine law y = β·u is ≥ jump/2 = {C.fr(jmax / 2)}')
print(f'  period formula vs beat-by-beat simulation (count over 240 beats = ⌊240/n(u)⌋): {bad} disagreements.')
print('\n5. MC5-ε is necessary (Theorem 15-2): identity Φ (laws, states, events, routes, interventions all the same), only ε changed')
hN = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE, C.NONE, ('A', (0, 0))]
for eps in (F(1, 100), F(1, 1000)):
    GK.EPS = eps
    DC.EPS = eps
    P, H, rows, I = C.snn_full('N', G, hN, 1, T=[('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0), ('w', 2, 0), ('w', 3, 0)])
    print(f'  Instance N (Chapter 11, 7.5 (2)), T = the four positions of T_max, ε = {C.fr(eps)}: Part = {P}, HDyn = {H}; events acting on T: '
          + '; '.join(f'{a} c={C.fr(c)} a={aa} η={v}' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows if a.startswith(('PLUS', 'MINUS'))))
GK.EPS = DC.EPS = F(1, 100)
print('\n6. MC4 intervention commutation (Theorem 15-11)')
hP = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
img = C.tau(hP)
W = set(range(26, 44))
for lab, sw1, sw2 in (('bap off (within W) ↦ cut(N→syn)', {t: (True, False) for t in W}, {t: {'cut'} for t in W}),
                      ('bap off ↦ cut(N→syn) ∧ frozen decay (corrected φ_I)', {t: (True, False) for t in W}, {t: {'cut', 'freeze_rl'} for t in W}),
                      ('plastic off (within W) ↦ freeze', {t: (False, True) for t in W}, {t: {'freeze'} for t in W})):
    L1 = C.snn_light(G, hP, 26, sw=sw1)

    hd1 = 0 if not L1['teff'] else 'judged separately'
    print(f'  {lab}: SNN has {len(L1["teff"])} effective in-window events acting on T ⇒ HDyn = {hd1}, Part (borne by SYN) = {L1["kappa_syn"]}, q = {L1["q"]} | '
          f'Family E HDyn = {r2["HDyn"]} (witnesses {r2["witness"][:2]}), Part = {r2["Part"]}, Ret = {r2["Ret"]}')
L1 = C.snn_light(G, hP[:26] + hP[26:], 26)
o26 = L1['pre'][26]
hE = hP
pre1, evs1, _ = C.snn_run(G, hE[:26])
oE = GK.setw(pre1[26], {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
teffE, o = [], oE
for t in range(26, 44):
    e = RW.Ev(G, o, hE[t], t)

    o, _ = G.step(o, hE[t])
r2E = C.lf_eval(C.WU, img, (26, 43), {0}, erase={26: (0,)})
print(f'  erasure (before beat 26, w00, w10 ← b ↦ z1 ← 0): SNN has {len(teffE)} effective in-window events acting on T (HDyn = 0), z at window end = {C.fx(o[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0])} | '
      f'Family E HDyn = {r2E["HDyn"]} (witnesses {r2E["witness"][:3]}), Ret = {r2E["Ret"]}, z1 re-formed at beat 26 by the constant term b of hebb: formation record {r2E["form"][0][-2:]}')
print('\n7. Insufficiency of actual-run correspondence (Theorem 15-4)')
M0 = dict(C.WU, gam=F(0))
h7 = ['pair', 'rest', 'drive', 'drive']
a = C.lf_eval(M0, h7, (2, 3), {0})
bs = {e['t']: e['c'] for e in a['wev'] if e['kind'] == 'hebb' and e['i'] == 0}
Mc = dict(M0, bsched=bs)
b = C.lf_eval(Mc, h7, (2, 3), {0})
print(f'  (A) MC2-r: Family E (γ = 0) and the variant in which hebb is replaced by a beat-wise constant law (c set to the actual values): z1 trajectories identical {a["traj"] == b["traj"] or [x[0] for x in a["traj"]] == [x[0] for x in b["traj"]]}, '
      f'MC1, MC3, MC5 hold; HDyn = {a["HDyn"]} and {b["HDyn"]} (the reading, in the transfer framework, of the HistCond example of Theorem 1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization*: inherited, not counted as an increment).')
I = res['S_A'][2]
ok_all = True
for u in sorted(set(I.avail[('w', 0, 0)]) & set(range(1, 27))):
    pin = GK.setw(I.pre[u], {k: G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
    _, _, g2 = GK.run_with(G, pin, I.hist[u:29], t_start=u)
    ok_all &= all(g2[t] == I.gates[t] for t in g2)

      f' ⇒ by Lemma C of Chapter 11 no setting in the upward-only domain changes the gates, PLUS and MINUS are unchanged, η = 0; under the original domain [b, hi], HDyn = {res["S_A"][1]}.')
print('\n8. Unidirectionality (P15-2): when φ_E is injective but not surjective, only the positive value of HDyn is preserved')
M1 = dict(C.WU, gam=F(0))

x2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, ['pair', 'rest', 'rest'], (1, 2), {0})

      'the inverse map φ_E⁻¹ is not total, so Pres⁻ cannot be obtained from Pres⁺ by inversion.')
print('\n9. Composition, inversion and scale conjugacy (positive witnesses of Theorems 15-1–15-3)')
ren = {'seed': 'pair', 'flow': 'drive', 'rest': 'rest'}
tot = eq = 0
for L in range(1, 5):
    for h9 in itertools.product(('seed', 'flow', 'rest'), repeat=L):

print(f'  scale conjugacy on 𝒦_g (w, b, lo, hi, θ, v_reset, A₊, A₋, teaching current and ε all multiplied by α = 2; x, y unchanged): Instance P_α has q = {qa}, r = {ra}, Part = {Pa}, HDyn = {Ha}; '
      f'η = 1 for every event acting on T: {all(r[3] == 1 for r in rowsa)}; weights at beats 0–26 scaled by α {conj}.')
print('  a valid non-homogeneous m: m(d) = min + (max − min)²/(1 + max − min) (idempotent, intermediate, symmetric), d = (0, 1/2): '
      f'm(2d) = {C.fr(F(1)**2 / (1 + 1))} ≠ 2·m(d) = {C.fr(2 * (F(1, 2)**2 / (1 + F(1, 2))))} — under the scale map “the same m” does not commute; the transfer of dynamics must be stated in terms of c* sets (Theorem 15-12).')
~~~

A.13  run15\_dyn.py  Run entry: checks of the additional conditions for the transfer of dynamics on the main pair (10.12)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: check of the transfer conditions for the dynamics conclusions D, B_m, F (Theorem 15-12) on the main pair.
Run: python3 -B run15_dyn.py > run15_dyn.txt
SNN side as in Chapter 12: bifurcation reading (D4), K = {path 0 = {w00, w10}, path 1 = {w01, w11}}, mean aggregation, valid write-back R-shift (when s1 is uniform within paths, this is w := b + c* for every synapse),
v, x, y, inputs, teaching currents and switches frozen; opening event = entries 0 and 1 open together and g_j = 1. Family E side as in Chapter 12, 4.1: z₁ = z₂ = c* (the R-pool follows and does not affect the formal reading).
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, RW, GK, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
S0 = GC.load_snn_core()[0]
G = GC.snn_gate(S0)
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * 6
REST = [C.NONE] * 8
HP = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
K = [[(0, 0), (1, 0)], [(0, 1), (1, 1)]]

def snn_readings(G, o, hist, t0, t1, c=None):
    if c is not None:
        o = GK.setw(o, {('w', i, j): G.b[i][j] + c for P in K for (i, j) in P})
    first, cnt = None, [0, 0]
    opened_first = set()
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        s = G.pat[hist[t][0]]
        o2, rec = G.step(o, hist[t])
        if s[0] and s[1]:
            op = {j for j in (0, 1) if rec['g'][j]}
            for j in op:
                cnt[j] += 1
            if op and first is None:
                first, opened_first = t, op
        o = o2
    share = None if sum(cnt) == 0 else tuple(F(x, sum(cnt)) for x in cnt)
    return frozenset(opened_first), share, cnt

def depth(o, P):
    return sum(o[0][i][j] - G.b[i][j] for (i, j) in P) / len(P)

pre, evs, gates = C.snn_run(G, HP)
o26 = pre[26]
d = [depth(o26, P) for P in K]
print('\n1. D: path depths at beat 26 (mean aggregation)', [C.fx(x) for x in d], '⇒ D₁ =', int(d[0] != d[1]))
img = C.tau(HP)                       # frozen transducer τ (D15-5)
r2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, img, (26, 43), {0})
z26 = r2['traj'][26]
print('  Family E image (h₂′, first pair only): z at beat 26 =', [C.fx(x) for x in z26], '⇒ D₂ =', int(z26[0] != z26[1]))
print('  The aggregation scheme is load-bearing: path 0 = (0, 1/2), path 1 = (1/4, 1/4): mean aggregation gives path depths (1/4, 1/4), D = 0; bottleneck aggregation gives (0, 1/4), D = 1; '
      'so transferring D requires aggregation correspondence (DT1) and one and the same order-preserving depth map for every member (different scales across members can map equal depths to unequal ones).')
print('\n2. B_m: reading type, domain of definition and the c* map (bifurcation K, W = beats 26–43, actual s1 run)')
Y0, sh0, cnt0 = snn_readings(G, o26, HP, 26, 43)
print(f'  original reading: Y_first = {set(Y0)}, share = {None if sh0 is None else tuple(C.fr(x) for x in sh0)}, opening counts {cnt0}')
grid = [F(0), d[0] / 8, d[0] / 7, d[0] / 4, d[0] / 2, 3 * d[0] / 4, d[0]]
for c in grid:
    Y1, sh1, cnt1 = snn_readings(G, o26, HP, 26, 43, c)
    Bf = int(Y1 != Y0)
    Bs = 'reading undefined' if sh1 is None else int(sh1 != sh0)
    print(f'  c* = {C.fx(c)}: Y_first = {set(Y1)} (B^first = {Bf}), share {None if sh1 is None else tuple(C.fx(x, 3) for x in sh1)} (B^share = {Bs})')

def wu_share(z, hist, t0, t1):
    tot = [F(0), F(0)]
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        if hist[t] == 'drive':
            for i in (0, 1):
                tot[i] += C.WU['b'] * 10 * (1 + z[i])          # y_i = β(w₀ + z_i)x, β = w₀ = x = 1
        z = [z[i] + (C.WU['b'] + C.WU['k'] * z[i] if hist[t] == 'drive' else 0) - C.WU['gam'] * z[i] for i in (0, 1)]
    return None if sum(tot) == 0 else tuple(x / sum(tot) for x in tot)

s0 = wu_share(z26, img, 26, 43)
print(f'  Family E image: original share {tuple(C.fx(x, 3) for x in s0)}; the first-opened and order-of-precedence readings have no object (no outflow threshold, Chapter 12, 3.2)')
for c in (min(z26), sum(z26) / 2, max(z26)):
    s1 = wu_share([c, c], img, 26, 43)
    print(f'    c*₂ = {C.fx(c)}: share {tuple(C.fx(x, 3) for x in s1)}, B^share = {int(s1 != s0)}')
print('  Conclusion: Y_first has no object in Family E (DT3 fails, B^first does not transfer); where Y_share is defined on both sides its values agree, but at small c* the SNN reading is undefined while Family E is always defined — '
      'the domains of definition of the readings do not correspond, and the “for every valid m” convention does not transfer.')
print('\n3. F and the Familiar Route (correspondence of Pass)')
for lab, W0, n in (('s1 (Instance P)', F(1, 4), 6), ('variant with 1 formation', F(1, 4), 1), ('W0 = 1/8, 1 formation', F(1, 8), 1)):
    S0.W0 = W0
    Gx = GC.snn_gate(S0)
    h = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * n + [C.NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
    t0 = 3 * n + 8
    prx, _, _ = C.snn_run(Gx, h)
    _, _, cnt = snn_readings(Gx, prx[t0], h, t0, len(h) - 1)
    imgx = C.tau(h)
    pass2 = sum(1 for t in range(t0, len(h)) if imgx[t] == 'drive')
    print(f'  {lab}: SNN path 0 in-window opening count Pass₁ = {cnt[0]}; Family E image Pass₂ (number of beats with y₁ > 0) = {pass2}; with habitual-travel criterion N = 3: Familiar Route {int(cnt[0] >= 3)} vs {int(pass2 >= 3)}')
S0.W0 = F(1, 4)
print('  F ⇒ Familiar Route (Chapter 12, fragment B7), so F does not transfer where the Familiar-Route values differ; the counting objects of Pass (opening events vs conducting beats) must correspond (DT5).')
print('\n4. Validity of I_m (citing Theorems C-I and W-2 of Chapter 12)')
print(f'  SNN: state rewriting in the model mode of the program host; s1 is uniform within paths at beat 26, C_leg = [min, max] ∩ ℚ = [0, {C.fx(d[0])}] ∩ ℚ. '
      'Family E: formally well-defined and unique; resource-feasible under R-pool ⇔ c* ≤ minᵢ(zᵢ + g₁Rᵢ); physical setting route undeclared. '
      'When φ_I maps the I_m of the SNN to the I_m of Family E, validity is maintained at the formal layer (both sides are well-defined) but not at the physical layer (the physical branch of DT2 fails).')
~~~

A.14  run15\_regress.py  Run entry: whole-step commutation under aggregation, graph structure, the Part route, effective action, the transducer and fibres, domain partition of the pure class with fixed n, the gating endpoint (10.1, 10.4, 10.5, 10.8, 10.9, 10.15)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: witnesses and positive controls for whole-step commutation under aggregation, graph structure, the Part route, effective action, transducers and fibres, the domain partition of pure classes with fixed n, and the endpoint of the ≥ gate.
Run: python3 run15_regress.py
"""
import sys, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, GK, banner
import core15 as C
t_start = time.time()
banner()
G = GC.snn_gate(GC.load_snn_core()[0])
N = C.NONE
A = ('A', (0, 0))
FORM = [C.FORM_LETTER, N, N]
T = C.TPOS
WU = C.WU
print('\n1. Aggregation and whole-step commutation (Theorem 15-1)')
print('  Algebraic example: two source positions start from (0, 0), and two constant writes add (1, 0) and (0, 1) respectively; one target position, where each of the two corresponding processes adds 1. '
      f'Bottleneck-aggregated source post-state min(1, 1) = {min(F(1), F(1))}, target post-state 2: the event-wise condition holds but whole-step commutation fails, so this is not admitted as RC.')
cnt = dict(tot=0, mean_ok=0, min_ok=0, q_div=0)
for L in range(1, 5):
    for h in itertools.product(('pair', 'drive', 'rest'), repeat=L):
        for asp in ((F(2), F(2)), (F(2), F(0)), (F(3), F(1))):
            xs = [F(0)] * 4
            zm = [F(0), F(0)]
            zb = [F(0), F(0)]
            okm = okb = True
            for letter in h:
                xs, _ = C.split_step(WU, xs, letter, asp)
                zm, _ = C.wu_mech_step(WU, zm, letter, (asp[0] + asp[1]) / 2)
                zb, _ = C.wu_mech_step(WU, zb, letter, min(asp))
                okm &= ((xs[0] + xs[1]) / 2 == zm[0] and (xs[2] + xs[3]) / 2 == zm[1])
                okb &= (min(xs[0], xs[1]) == zb[0] and min(xs[2], xs[3]) == zb[1])
            cnt['tot'] += 1
            cnt['mean_ok'] += okm
            cnt['min_ok'] += okb
            eps = WU['eps']
            cnt['q_div'] += (min(xs[0], xs[1]) <= eps < (xs[0] + xs[1]) / 2)
print(f'  Positive control for a valid nontrivial linear aggregation: each synapse of Family E is split into two parallel sub-synapses with the same law (four-position source; the laws b + k·z, −γ·z read their own position), path-mean aggregation ↦ Family E, '
      f'formation pulse split as (2,2), (2,0), (3,1); all histories of length 1–4 × 3 splits, {cnt["tot"]} groups in all; mechanism-level whole-step commutation (path mean = Family E state) holds in {cnt["mean_ok"]} groups.')
print(f'  Same source with bottleneck aggregation (min) as the correspondence and the target pulse taken as the min of the split: whole-step commutation holds in {cnt["min_ok"]}/{cnt["tot"]} groups — in this source every sub-synapse within a path undergoes the same strictly increasing affine map at each beat, '
      'and the pulse split does not change the order within a path, so min commutes with it; this is one case of MC2^step verified case by case for a nonlinear aggregation. In the algebraic example the two members receive different events and commutation fails. Bottleneck aggregation does not inherit the event induction of linear sums; whole-step commutation can only be verified case by case.')
print(f'  Limit on atoms under aggregation: {cnt["q_div"]} groups whose final state has one of the two sub-synapses in a path ≤ ε while the path mean is > ε — RC under mean aggregation does not give preservation of position-wise atoms (q, availability); a within-path agreement condition must be added (Theorem 15-6).')
print('\n2. Numerical conjugacy does not include reads or graph structure (Theorem 15-3)')
h2 = ['pair', 'rest', 'drive', 'drive']
M0 = dict(WU, gam=F(0))
a = C.lf_eval(M0, h2, (2, 3), {0})
b = C.lf_eval(M0, h2, (2, 3), {0}, sw={2: {'cut'}, 3: {'cut'}})
print(f'  Family E (γ = 0) vs the same model with the N→syn segment missing from the declared route chain of hebb: law, occurrence, components and trajectory identical beat by beat (numerical conjugacy MC2⁺ holds) {a["traj"] == b["traj"]}; '
      f'HDyn = {a["HDyn"]} and {b["HDyn"]}. So MC2⁺ does not imply MC2-r or the graph condition MC-G; both are listed as independent antecedents.')
print('\n3. The Part route (Theorem 15-5)')
hP = FORM * 6 + [N] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
W = set(range(26, 44))
L1 = C.snn_light(G, hP, 26, sw={t: (False, True) for t in W})
img = C.tau(hP)
r2 = C.lf_eval(WU, img, (26, 43), {0}, sw={t: {'freeze'} for t in W})
print(f'  Frozen control: source Part = {L1["kappa_syn"]} (borne by SYN), {len(L1["teff"])} effective events in the source window acting on T (HDyn₁ = 0); target Part = {r2["Part"]}, HDyn = {r2["HDyn"]}. '
      'The antecedent of Theorem 15-5, “source HDyn₁ = 1”, fails, so this example lies outside it; no general preservation of Part is issued.')
Part1, HDyn1, rows1, I1 = C.snn_full('P', G, hP, 26)
r2p = C.lf_eval(WU, img, (26, 43), {0})
print(f'  Positive control (Instance P, no intervention): source HDyn₁ = {HDyn1}, target HDyn₂ = {r2p["HDyn"]}, target Part₂ = {r2p["Part"]} (the witness carrier of HDyn₂ lies inside B, Supp_act ⊆ Supp_cond).')
print('\n4. Availability is not effective action (Theorem 15-9)')
r3 = C.lf_eval(WU, ['pair'] + ['rest'] * 100, (100, 100), {0})
e3 = [e for e in r3['wev'] if e['i'] == 0][0]
print(f'  Long-rest example: Family E with original parameters, pair followed by rest×100, W = [100, 100]: z1 available {e3["avail"]}, HistCond of decay = {e3["hist"]}, |c| = {C.fx(abs(e3["c"]))} ≤ ε₂, Act = {e3["a"]}, HDyn = {r3["HDyn"]}. '
      'Eff₂ (some bearing event in the window acting on z1 has |c| > ε₂ and z1 is available at that beat) fails, so this is not in the domain where HDyn ≡ 1; HDyn = 0 holds only for this window and this T.')
eff = [e for e in r2p['wev'] if e['i'] == 0 and e['a'] and e['avail']]
print(f'  Positive control (main image τ(h₁), W = beats 26–43): Eff₂ holds ({len(eff)} effective events acting on z1 while it is available, the first {eff[0]["kind"]}@{eff[0]["t"]}), HDyn₂ = {r2p["HDyn"]}.')
print('\n5. Frozen transducer, fibres and the substantive-transfer domains on pure classes of the main pair (Theorems 15-8, 15-15)')
print(f'  Transducer example: for letters that are all (A, teaching (1, 0)), τ outputs pair or rest depending on its state f: τ(formation×2) = {C.tau([C.FORM_LETTER, C.FORM_LETTER])}; τ is a Mealy machine (a deterministic sequential function), not a static letter function. '
      'The image history that maps only the first formation letter to pair is identical to τ(h₁) beat by beat (assertion in run15_main).')
P_AB3 = FORM * 6 + [N] * 8 + [('AB', (0, 0)) if x[0] == 'A' else x for x in GC.WIN0()]
Pab, Hab, _, _ = C.snn_full('P_AB3', G, P_AB3, 26)
print(f'  τ-fibre of Instance P (class admitting AB): the member with the three A beats in the window changed to AB has the same τ-image as P {C.tau(P_AB3) == C.tau(hP)}, HDyn = {Hab} (P has {HDyn1}) — this fibre is not pure.')
print('  Pure class 𝒞₁ᵖ(n) (n is protocol data fixed in advance for each declared class): s1 parameters; the letters are only the formation letter (A, (1, 0)), (A, 0) and rest; n formations (beats 0, 3, …), k rest beats, and the window W is the 3 beats that follow.')
rows = []
decl = {}
for n in (1, 3, 6):
    for k in (0, 8, 60):
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=3):
            h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
            t0 = 3 * n + k
            img = tuple(C.tau(h))
            decl.setdefault((n, img, t0), set()).add((n, k, wv))
            Lx = C.snn_light(G, h, t0)
            r2x = C.lf_eval(WU, list(img), (t0, t0 + 2), {0})
            gs_ticks = [t for t in range(t0, t0 + 3) if h[t] == A and
                        C.gs(G, Lx['pre'][t], h[t], T, {kk for kk in T if t in Lx['avail'][kk]})[0]
                        and any(t in Lx['avail'][kk] for kk in T)]
            z1 = r2x['traj']
            pi1 = any(img[t] == 'drive' and z1[t][0] > WU['eps'] for t in gs_ticks)
            noA = all(x == N for x in wv)
            dec_eff = any(WU['gam'] * z1[t][0] > WU['eps'] and z1[t][0] > WU['eps'] for t in range(t0, t0 + 3))
            fired = any(Lx['gates'][t][0] for t in range(t0, t0 + 3))
            cls = 'I' if pi1 else ('II' if noA and not dec_eff else ('III' if noA else ('IV⁰' if not fired else 'IV*')))
            P1v, H1v, _, _ = C.snn_full('c', G, h, t0)
            rows.append((n, k, wv, cls, H1v, r2x['HDyn']))
inj = all(len(v) == 1 for v in decl.values())
print(f'  τ injective within a class of fixed n (the fibre data include n and the window W): {inj}; {len(rows)} declarations in all. Without fixing n, τ is not injective (run15_pure, Part 3).')
for cls in ('I', 'II', 'III', 'IV⁰', 'IV*'):
    sub = [r for r in rows if r[3] == cls]
    pres = sum(1 for r in sub if r[4] == r[5])
    vals = sorted({(r[4], r[5]) for r in sub})
    print(f'  domain Π_{cls}: {len(sub)}; with HDyn₁ = HDyn₂: {pres}; (HDyn₁, HDyn₂) occurring: {vals}')
print('  Reading: on Π_I ∪ Π_II HDyn is preserved and both values occur (substantive transfer, Theorem 15-15); on Π_III, 0 vs 1, not preserved (Family E decay bears HDyn in rest windows, while s1 has ρ = 0 and no corresponding process); '
      'on Π_IV⁰ (no A beat in the window fires), 0 vs 1, not preserved (the Family E drive write has a constant term b that does not read the Trace-Response, while the SNN must cross the threshold); '
      'Π_IV* (firing but no GS beat) case by case (evaluated by the Chapter 11 adjudicator), not issued at family level.')
for r in rows:
    if r[3] == 'IV*':
        print(f'    Π_IV*: n = {r[0]}, k = {r[1]}, W = {"".join("A" if x == A else "·" for x in r[2])}: HDyn₁ = {r[4]}, HDyn₂ = {r[5]}')
print('\n6. The endpoint of the ≥ gate')
h = FORM * 6 + [N] * 8 + [('A', (F(1, 2), 0)), N, N]
pre, _, _ = C.snn_run(G, h)
ok, vt, vtb = C.gs(G, pre[26], h[26], T, set(T))
print(f'  S_I beat 26: gate argument with T set back to baseline = {C.fr(vtb)} = θ (the minimum endpoint is exactly the threshold), actual {C.fx(vt)}; GS = {ok}: the gate is open under every setting, so this is not an FD witness. '
      'A same-beat flip requires min vt < θ ≤ max vt.')
~~~

A.15  run15\_pure.py  Run entry: effective-booking truncation and aggregation, necessity of event-wise conjugacy, the variable-n cross test and domain partition of the pure class with fixed n (10.1, 10.3, 10.4, 10.15)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: F-layer effective-booking truncation and aggregation, the necessity of event-wise conjugacy, the variable-n cross test and the domain partition of pure classes with fixed n.
Run: python3 run15_pure.py
Part 1, F-layer effective-booking truncation and aggregation (F branch of Lemma 15-1a); Part 2, necessity of event-wise conjugacy (Theorem 15-3);
Part 3, cross test of variable n and compensating k, injectivity of τ within classes of fixed n, and domain partition (Theorem 15-15).
"""
import sys, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
WU = C.WU
print('\n1. F-layer effective booking Q(c) = c·[|c| > ε], and linear aggregation')
eps = F(1, 100)
cs = [F(3, 500), F(7, 500)]
print(f'  Mixed-threshold example: c = (3/500, 7/500), path mean, ε = 1/100: raw target component {C.fr(sum(cs) / 2)} = ε, Q₂ = {C.fr(C.Qe(sum(cs) / 2, eps))}; '
      f'effective source mean {C.fr(sum(C.Qe(c, eps) for c in cs) / 2)}. Same-branch condition CQ = {C.cq_condition(cs, eps)}: excluded by the F branch of Lemma 15-1a.')
vals = [F(-3, 100), F(-1, 100), F(-1, 200), F(0), F(1, 200), F(3, 500), F(1, 100), F(7, 500), F(3, 100)]
stat = {}
bad_cq = 0
for wts in ((F(1, 2), F(1, 2)), (F(1, 3), F(2, 3)), (F(1, 4), F(3, 4))):
    for al in (F(1), F(2)):
        for c1, c2 in itertools.product(vals, repeat=2):
            lhs = C.Qe(al * (wts[0] * c1 + wts[1] * c2), al * eps)
            rhs = al * (wts[0] * C.Qe(c1, eps) + wts[1] * C.Qe(c2, eps))
            cq = C.cq_condition([c1, c2], eps)
            if abs(c1) <= eps and abs(c2) <= eps:
                kind = 'under threshold (incl. equality)'
            elif abs(c1) > eps and abs(c2) > eps:
                kind = 'suprathreshold'
            else:
                kind = 'cross-threshold'
            if eps in (abs(c1), abs(c2)):
                kind += '·equality case'
            d = stat.setdefault(kind, [0, 0, 0])
            d[0] += 1
            d[1] += (lhs == rhs)
            d[2] += cq
            if cq and lhs != rhs:
                bad_cq += 1
for kind, (n_, ok, ncq) in sorted(stat.items()):
    print(f'  {kind}: {n_} cases, Q commutes with the weighted sum in {ok}, CQ satisfied in {ncq}')
print(f'  Three sets of convex weights × α ∈ {{1, 2}} (ε₂ = α·ε₁) × 81 component pairs: {bad_cq} cases where CQ holds but commutation fails (finite verification of Lemma 15-1a (F)).')
# F-layer positive control: four-position split source (F truncation) vs Family E (F truncation)
tot = ok_all = cq_all = cq_ok = 0
e1 = WU['eps']
for L in range(1, 5):
    for h in itertools.product(('pair', 'drive', 'rest'), repeat=L):
        for asp in ((F(2), F(2)), (F(2), F(0)), (F(3), F(1))):
            xs = [F(0)] * 4
            zs = [F(0)] * 2
            good, cqh = True, True
            for letter in h:
                _, csx = C.split_step(WU, xs, letter, asp)
                _, csz = C.wu_mech_step(WU, zs, letter, (asp[0] + asp[1]) / 2)
                for p in range(2):
                    for j in range(len(csz[p])):
                        fam = [csx[2 * p][j] if j < len(csx[2 * p]) else F(0), csx[2 * p + 1][j] if j < len(csx[2 * p + 1]) else F(0)]
                        cqh &= C.cq_condition(fam, e1)
                xs = [x + sum(C.Qe(c, e1) for c in cx) for x, cx in zip(xs, csx)]
                zs = [z + sum(C.Qe(c, e1) for c in cz) for z, cz in zip(zs, csz)]
                good &= ((xs[0] + xs[1]) / 2 == zs[0] and (xs[2] + xs[3]) / 2 == zs[1])
            tot += 1
            ok_all += good
            cq_all += cqh
            cq_ok += (cqh and good)
print(f'  F-layer positive control: four-position split source ↦ Family E (both sides booked with F truncation, ε = 1/1000): of {tot} groups, whole-step commutation holds in {ok_all}; '
      f'CQ holds throughout, beat by beat and family by family, in {cq_all} groups, of which commutation holds in {cq_ok} (CQ ⇒ commutation). At the mechanism level all 360 groups commute (run15_regress, Part 1); the F layer needs CQ in addition.')
print('\n2. State conjugacy and graph isomorphism do not give event-wise amplitude conjugacy')
x = F(1)
eps2 = F(3, 20)
src = (x / 10, -x / 10)
dst = (x / 5, -x / 5)
print(f'  Cancellation example: one position x = 1, two in-boundary processes that always occur and read their own position, source components (+x/10, −x/10), target (+x/5, −x/5), Φ the identity, ε = 3/20: '
      f'net increments {C.fr(sum(src))} and {C.fr(sum(dst))} on the two sides, MC2^step holds on the whole domain, the slot graphs are isomorphic; source Act = {[int(abs(c) > eps2) for c in src]}, target Act = {[int(abs(c) > eps2) for c in dst]}, '
      f'HDyn 0 vs 1. Event-wise amplitude conjugacy MC2⁺(Δ_𝒟) (c₂ = α·c₁, α = 1) fails: Theorem 15-3 takes MC2⁺(Δ_𝒟) as an antecedent, so this example is excluded.')
a = C.lf_eval(WU, ['pair', 'rest', 'drive', 'drive', 'rest'], (2, 4), {0})
b = C.lf_eval(WU, ['pair', 'rest', 'drive', 'drive', 'rest'], (2, 4), {0}, eps=WU['eps'] * 2, scale=F(2))
print(f'  Positive control (MC2⁺ holds): Family E vs Family E_α (α = 2; a, b and ε all multiplied by α): HDyn {a["HDyn"]} and {b["HDyn"]}, per-event Act identical '
      f'{[e["a"] for e in a["wev"]] == [e["a"] for e in b["wev"]]}, HistCond identical {[e["hist"] for e in a["wev"]] == [e["hist"] for e in b["wev"]]}.')
print('\n3. Cross test of variable n and compensating k; injectivity and domain partition within classes of fixed n')
G = GC.snn_gate(GC.load_snn_core()[0])
N = C.NONE
A = ('A', (0, 0))
FORM = [C.FORM_LETTER, N, N]
groups = {}
for n in range(1, 7):
    for k in range(0, 21):
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=3):
            h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
            groups.setdefault((tuple(C.tau(h)), 3 * n + k), set()).add((n, k, wv))
coll = [v for v in groups.values() if len({d[0] for d in v}) > 1]
print(f'  n ∈ 1–6 × k ∈ 0–20 × 8 kinds of window ({sum(len(v) for v in groups.values())} declarations): {len(coll)} groups in which one (τ-image, window) contains different n — without fixing n, τ is not injective.')
for n, k in ((1, 15), (6, 0)):
    h = FORM * n + [N] * k + [A]
    L = C.snn_light(G, h, 18)
    g = C.gs(G, L['pre'][18], A, C.TPOS, {kk for kk in C.TPOS if 18 in L['avail'][kk]})[0]
    P_, H_, _, _ = C.snn_full('compensation example', G, h, 18)
    r2 = C.lf_eval(WU, C.tau(h), (18, 18), {0})
    print(f'  Compensation example: n = {n}, k = {k}, W = [18, 18]: τ-image = pair·rest^17·drive {C.tau(h) == ["pair"] + ["rest"] * 17 + ["drive"]}; source GS = {g}, HDyn₁ = {H_}; target HDyn₂ = {r2["HDyn"]}. '
          f'The two histories belong to 𝒞₁ᵖ(1) and 𝒞₁ᵖ(6) respectively: the former lies in Π_IV⁰ (0 vs 1, not preserved), the latter in Π_I (1 vs 1).')
inj = True
for n in range(1, 7):
    seen = {}
    for k in range(0, 71):
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=3):
            h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
            key = (tuple(C.tau(h)), 3 * n + k)
            if key in seen and seen[key] != (k, wv):
                inj = False
            seen[key] = (k, wv)
print(f'  Fixed n (each class 1–6) × k ∈ 0–70 × 8 kinds of window: (τ-image, window) pairwise distinct within each class {inj} (consistent with the proof of Theorem 15-15 (1)).')
rows = []
for n in (1, 3, 6):
    for k in list(range(0, 21)) + [30, 45, 60]:
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=3):
            h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
            t0 = 3 * n + k
            img = C.tau(h)
            Lx = C.snn_light(G, h, t0)
            z1 = C.lf_eval(WU, img, (t0, t0 + 2), {0})
            gs_t = [t for t in range(t0, t0 + 3) if h[t] == A and any(t in Lx['avail'][kk] for kk in C.TPOS)
                    and C.gs(G, Lx['pre'][t], h[t], C.TPOS, {kk for kk in C.TPOS if t in Lx['avail'][kk]})[0]]
            pi1 = any(z1['traj'][t][0] > WU['eps'] for t in gs_t)
            noA = all(x == N for x in wv)
            dec = any(WU['gam'] * z1['traj'][t][0] > WU['eps'] and z1['traj'][t][0] > WU['eps'] for t in range(t0, t0 + 3))
            fired = any(Lx['gates'][t][0] for t in range(t0, t0 + 3))
            cls = 'I' if pi1 else ('II' if noA and not dec else ('III' if noA else ('IV⁰' if not fired else 'IV*')))
            rows.append((n, k, wv, cls, h, t0, z1['HDyn']))
print(f'  Domain partition (n ∈ {{1, 3, 6}}, k ∈ 0–20 ∪ {{30, 45, 60}}, 8 kinds of window, {len(rows)} declarations in all; for Π_I–Π_IV⁰ the source values come from the proof of Theorem 15-15, while Π_IV* and the spot checks use the Chapter 11 adjudicator):')
check = {}
first = {}
for i, r in enumerate(rows):
    first.setdefault(r[3], []).append(i)
pick = set(i for v in first.values() for i in v[:8])
for i, r in enumerate(rows):
    if r[3] == 'IV*' or i % 12 == 0 or i in pick:
        P_, H_, _, _ = C.snn_full('c', G, r[4], r[5])
        check[i] = H_
proof = {'I': 1, 'II': 0, 'III': 0, 'IV⁰': 0}
viol = sum(1 for i, h1 in check.items() if rows[i][3] in proof and proof[rows[i][3]] != h1)
for cls in ('I', 'II', 'III', 'IV⁰', 'IV*'):
    sub = [(i, r) for i, r in enumerate(rows) if r[3] == cls]
    tgt = sorted({r[6] for _, r in sub})
    if cls in proof:
        print(f'    Π_{cls}: {len(sub)}; source HDyn₁ = {proof[cls]} (proved), target HDyn₂ ∈ {tgt}; adjudicator spot checks: {sum(1 for i, _ in sub if i in check)}')
    else:
        pairs = {}
        for i, r in sub:
            pairs[(check[i], r[6])] = pairs.get((check[i], r[6]), 0) + 1
        print(f'    Π_IV*: {len(sub)} (all by the adjudicator); distribution of (HDyn₁, HDyn₂) {sorted(pairs.items())}')
print(f'  Spot checks disagreeing with the proved source values: {viol}.')
~~~
