A.16  run15\_sat.py  Run entry: clipping individuation and the decidable subdomain of Π\_I, negative-determination declarations and three-valued preservation, CQ not necessary, finite-dimensional yet with infinitely many states, the numbers of formations for which Π\_I is nonempty (10.1, 10.3, 10.15, 5.5)

~~~python
"""Appendix program of Chapter 15: clipping individuation and the decidable subdomain of Π_I, negative-determination declarations and three-valued preservation, CQ not necessary, finite-dimensional yet with infinitely many states.
Run: python3 run15_sat.py
The clipping examples only replay explicit same-beat positive certificates and call the three-valued composition (judge) of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0; they do not exhaust all branches of long histories. Where no certificate has been supplied, only ⊥_Cov is given, not 0.
"""
import sys, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, GK, DC, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
G = GC.snn_gate(GC.load_snn_core()[0])
N = C.NONE
A = ('A', (0, 0))
FORM = [C.FORM_LETTER, N, N]
T = C.TPOS

class PositiveCertificates:
    """Adjudicator stand-in holding only explicit same-beat positive certificates: fd1 holds the replayed event witnesses, fd1_net the net-increment witnesses of joint events."""
    def __init__(self):
        self.sat, self.fd1, self.fd1_net = {}, {}, {}
    def fd1_value(self, e):
        return 1 if e['id'] in self.fd1 else GK.COV

def t_clip(I, t):
    return [e for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in T]

def replay(n, k=0):
    h = FORM * n + [N] * k + [A]
    t = 3 * n + k
    I = GK.Instance('clipping control', G, h, t, T=T)
    o, actual = I.pre[t], I.evs[t]
    avail = {x for x in T if I.is_avail(x, t)}
    gs = C.gs(G, o, h[t], T, avail)[0]
    D = PositiveCertificates()
    for e in t_clip(I, t):
        x = e['tgt']
        D.sat[(x, t)] = I.pre[t + 1][0][x[1]][x[2]] - o[0][x[1]][x[2]]
    for val in (F(1, 4), F(1), F(3, 2)):
        asg = {x: val for x in avail}
        alt = GK.setw(o, asg)
        out, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, alt, h[t:], t_start=t)
        for e in actual.values():
            ee = evs[t].get(e['id'])
            if ee is None or ee['c'] != e['c']:
                D.fd1.setdefault(e['id'], (t, asg))
        for (x, tt), net0 in D.sat.items():
            if out[t + 1][0][x[1]][x[2]] - alt[0][x[1]][x[2]] != net0:
                D.fd1_net.setdefault((x, tt), (t, asg))
    r = DC.judge(I, D, True)
    img = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h), (t, t), {0})
    w_pre = [o[0][x[1]][x[2]] for x in T]
    return dict(t=t, gs=gs, clip=len(t_clip(I, t)), net=sorted(set(D.sat.values())), w_pre=w_pre,
                sep=r['HDyn_sep'], joint=r['HDyn_joint'], hd=r['HDyn'], hd2=img['HDyn'])

print('\n1. Clipping individuation and the decidable subdomain of Π_I (Theorem 15-15)')
first_touch = None
for n in range(1, 41):
    h = FORM * n + [A]
    pre, evs, _ = C.snn_run(G, h)
    cl = [e for e in evs[3 * n].values() if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in T]
    if cl and all(pre[3 * n][0][x[1]][x[2]] < 2 for x in T):
        first_touch = n
        break
for lab, n in (('entry already saturated (both positions at the upper bound before the window)', 30), ('first hit of the cap within the window (both positions below the upper bound before the window)', first_touch)):
    r = replay(n)
    print(f'  {lab}: n = {n}, k = 0, W = [{r["t"]}, {r["t"]}], only (A, 0) in the window; before the window (w00, w10) = ({C.fx(r["w_pre"][0])}, {C.fx(r["w_pre"][1])}); '
          f'GS = {r["gs"]}; {r["clip"]} clippings at T positions in the window, joint net increments {[C.fx(x) for x in r["net"]]}; '
          f'separate reading HDyn = {r["sep"]}, joint reading HDyn = {r["joint"]}, verdict HDyn₁ = {r["hd"]}; image HDyn₂ = {r["hd2"]}.')
print('  Neither example satisfies the no-clipping condition NC_W of Π_I, so neither lies in the domain issued as HDyn₁ = 1; ⊥ is not read as 0.')
cnt = sum_clip = 0
for n in (1, 3, 6):
    for k in list(range(0, 21)) + [30, 45, 60]:
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=3):
            h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
            t0 = 3 * n + k
            pre, evs, _ = C.snn_run(G, h)
            cnt += 1
            sum_clip += sum(1 for t in range(t0, t0 + 3) for e in evs[t].values() if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in T)
print(f'  Among the {cnt} declarations with n ∈ {{1, 3, 6}}, k ∈ 0–20 ∪ {{30, 45, 60}} and |W| = 3, clippings at T positions in the window total {sum_clip}: NC_W holds throughout, and the finite-verification counts of Theorem 15-15 are unchanged.')
print(f'  Number of formations at which clipping of T positions within the window first appears under the single-beat window (A, 0): n = {first_touch}.')
print('\n2. Negative-determination declarations and three-valued preservation (Theorem 15-3 (2))')
for lab, wl in (('S_AB', [('AB', (0, 0)), N, N]), ('S_A', [A, N, N])):
    h = FORM * 6 + [N] * 8 + wl
    I = GK.Instance(lab, G, h, 26, T=T)
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    yes, no = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'  {lab}, identity Φ (model, history, intervention domain and routes all identical): both sides declare B_FD: HDyn = {yes["HDyn"]} vs {yes["HDyn"]}; '
          f'neither declares: {no["HDyn"]} vs {no["HDyn"]}; source declares, target does not: {yes["HDyn"]} vs {no["HDyn"]}; Part under the two readings {yes["Part"]}, {no["Part"]}.')
print('  When the source declares and the target does not, the correspondence ND of negative-determination declarations fails, so the case is not within the antecedents of three-valued Pres; the undeclared side is recorded as ⊥, not changed to 0. Positive certificates (η = 1 of S_A) do not depend on negative-determination declarations and transfer under both declarations.')
print('\n3. CQ is not a necessary condition (Lemma 15-1a)')
eps = F(1, 100)
cs = (F(1, 50), -F(1, 50))
lhs = C.Qe(sum(cs) / 2, eps)
rhs = sum(C.Qe(c, eps) for c in cs) / 2
print(f'  c = (1/50, −1/50), mean, ε = 1/100: CQ = {C.cq_condition(list(cs), eps)}, Q(mean) = {C.fr(lhs)}, mean(Q) = {C.fr(rhs)}, commutes {lhs == rhs}.')
print('\n4. Finite-dimensional and deterministic, yet with infinitely many states (5.5)')
zs = [2 * F(9, 10) ** m for m in range(101)]
print(f'  Family E under R semantics, rest after pair, z1 = 2·(9/10)^m: first 101 states pairwise distinct {len(set(zs)) == 101}, strictly decreasing {all(zs[i] > zs[i + 1] for i in range(100))}.')
print('\n5. Formation counts with nonempty Π_I and the range of substantive transfer (Theorem 15-15 (2), (3))')

def pi1_member(n, k, wv):
    h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
    t0 = 3 * n + k
    L = C.snn_light(G, h, t0)
    img = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h), (t0, t0 + len(wv) - 1), {0})
    nc = not any(e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in T for t in range(t0, t0 + len(wv)) for e in L['evs'][t].values())
    for t in range(t0, t0 + len(wv)):
        av = {x for x in T if t in L['avail'][x]}
        if h[t] == A and av and C.gs(G, L['pre'][t], h[t], T, av)[0] and img['traj'][t][0] > C.WU['eps'] and nc:
            return True
    return False

wins = [wv for m in range(1, 4) for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=m)]
with_pi1, top = [], None
for n in range(1, 41):
    pre, _, _ = C.snn_run(G, FORM * n)
    if top is None and all(pre[3 * n][0][x[1]][x[2]] == 2 for x in T):
        top = n
    if any(pi1_member(n, k, wv) for k in (0, 1, 2, 4, 8) for wv in wins):
        with_pi1.append(n)
print(f'  n = 1–40, k ∈ {{0, 1, 2, 4, 8}}, all windows of length 1–3: the n with members of Π_I are {with_pi1}.')
print(f'  Smallest n for which both T positions are at the upper bound 2 after formation: {top}; for n = {top}–40 both positions are 2 after formation: '
      f'{all(all(C.snn_run(G, FORM * n)[0][3 * n][0][x[1]][x[2]] == 2 for x in T) for n in range(top, 41))}.')
ws = [C.snn_run(G, FORM * n)[0][3 * n][0][0][0] for n in range(1, 41)]
print(f'  After formation w00 = w10 (for n = 1–40 the two positions are identical beat by beat {all(C.snn_run(G, FORM * n)[0][3 * n][0][0][0] == C.snn_run(G, FORM * n)[0][3 * n][0][1][0] for n in range(1, 41))}), '
      f'nondecreasing in n {all(ws[i] <= ws[i + 1] for i in range(39))}; w(23) = {C.fx(ws[22])}, w(24) = {C.fx(ws[23])}, 2 − w(24) = {C.fx(2 - ws[23])} < 1/16: {2 - ws[23] < F(1, 16)}.')
emp = 0
tot = 0
for k in (0, 1, 8, 60):
    for m in range(1, 7):
        for wv in itertools.product((A, N), repeat=m):
            tot += 1
            emp += pi1_member(30, k, wv)
print(f'  n = 30: all windows with k ∈ {{0, 1, 8, 60}} and window length 1–6, {tot} cases in all, of which {emp} are members of Π_I (for the general proof see the main text: upper-bound invariant).')
h2 = FORM * 30 + [N] * 60 + [N]
L2 = C.snn_light(G, h2, 150)
r2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h2), (150, 150), {0})
print(f'  n = 30, k = 60, W = [150, 150], all rest: in Π_II, {len(L2["teff"])} effective events in the source window acting on T (HDyn₁ = 0), image HDyn₂ = {r2["HDyn"]} — this class has only zero values.')
for n in (with_pi1[0], with_pi1[-1]):
    for lab, k, wv in (('Π_I', None, None), ('Π_II', 60, (N,))):
        if lab == 'Π_I':
            k, wv = next((k, wv) for k in (0, 1, 2, 4, 8) for wv in wins if pi1_member(n, k, wv))
        h = FORM * n + [N] * k + list(wv)
        t0 = 3 * n + k
        P_, H_, _, _ = C.snn_full('witness', G, h, t0)
        rr = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h), (t0, t0 + len(wv) - 1), {0})
        print(f'  two-valued witness: n = {n}, {lab}, k = {k}, W = {"".join("A" if x == A else "·" for x in wv)}: adjudicator HDyn₁ = {H_}, image HDyn₂ = {rr["HDyn"]}')
~~~
